  camera rotation/view-to-model reads, direct MGED saved-view snapshot reads,
  direct MGED RT command eyepoint-correction view-to-model reads, and direct
  MGED view-ring save/autoview ring-scale snapshot reads plus direct MGED
  command autoview ring-scale reads, and direct MGED edsol
  absolute-translation, rotate-about, NMG/ARS/metaball, BoT/NMG edit-pick, and
  solid/object edit mouse-projection view snapshot reads, plus direct MGED
  dozoom perspective, eye-position, and model-to-view read snapshots, and
  direct libged inverse-size/keypoint/rotate-about query plus utility-helper
  reads, plus direct libged AET/eye-position/perspective setter readbacks and
  direct libged knob keypoint-origin plus size/scale/zoom setter readbacks,
  direct libged pure-rotation and MGED view-scale ratio readbacks,
  plus direct libged/TclCAD/MGED default/query coord, MGED edit-knob coord
  save, MGED cmd staging/cache/hash adapter-covered view reads, and direct
  MGED temp-staging/libged-DM fallback width/height reads, plus direct libged
  pmat/pmodel2view query output, TclCAD draw projection-matrix loads, MGED
  staging/cache/hash projection-matrix reads, direct initial/absolute scale
  readbacks in MGED cache/hash and knob query output, and direct
  unit-conversion stash reads in MGED cache and TclCAD refresh paths, plus
  direct MGED VIEWSIZE/VIEWFACTOR macro view-size/scale reads,
  direct qtcad camera/snap/input/sketch/selection read-side view snapshots and
  qtcad measurement, quad-view source-copy, and SW/GL fallback framebuffer or
  matrix-load view reads, plus direct controller-owned database-source
  replacement field assignments that would bypass the grouped configuration
  helper,
  rejects direct BSG mesh-LoD detail callbacks in the BRep LoD producer, and
  pins the RT source full-detail LoD provider contract, direct BoT database
  read path, source-request conversion/submission helpers, exact-result
  consumption helpers plus action-level submit/drain helpers for
  export/measure/snap and exact mesh pick request collection/consumption,
  qtcad source-backed exact pick, snap, and measure caller integration plus
  controller exact full-detail budget submission, multi-source exact
  database-source matching, duplicate-active exact source reload suppression,
  stale source-metrics behavior, and over-budget whole-object fallback coverage;
  strict mode and final allowlist removal remain.
* Low-level cleanup: 98 percent.  vlist, plot3, line-layer, RT view-info,
  neutral RT LoD policy snapshots, mesh-LoD handles, sketch polygons,
  normalized view-coordinate constants and
  view size/scale limits in selected libged/TclCAD/MGED command/helper paths,
  the private libged BSG-to-RT view adapter declaration and LoD policy
  snapshot/apply boundary for draw and LoD commands, and several utility paths
  including libged database-index LoD cache invalidation and view lod cache
  clear/status/BoT-refresh operations plus draw BoT LoD cache ownership are
  neutralized; active draw LoD publication now reads the RT mesh-LoD data view
  rather than BSG container fields; private BSG draw LoD adapters now take
  RT mesh-LoD handles instead of exposing borrowed BSG mesh pointers to
  draw.cpp; generated BRep mesh cache writes now go through RT helpers,
  draw source state no longer stores BSG mesh-LoD context or borrowed BSG mesh
  state, public GED no longer owns a BSG mesh-LoD context, and draw-ref reset
  now frees transitional scene-ref LoD state through the private adapter
  rather than including bsg/lod.h directly; draw tests now verify the
  transaction bounds-update hook through the same private adapter instead of
  naming bsg_view_bounds directly; the private libged view adapter now
  delegates BSG bounds-callback, view-scale, and scene-ref LoD load/free
  behavior to the RT legacy adapter instead of
  including bsg/lod.h; BRep full-detail callbacks now publish through neutral
  RT mesh-LoD detail callbacks instead of BSG callback signatures, so libged
  no longer includes bsg/lod.h for LoD work; qtcad quad views and MGED zoom/idle/attach
  paths no longer use direct BSG LoD policy structs, and qged view-info now
  reads size, dimensions, AET, and center through the RT legacy adapter, but
  lower-level framebuffer/libdm, BRep LoD tessellation producers, and
  some BSG scene paths remain.
* libimgstream migration batch: 57 percent.  Core streams, icv snapshots, and
  memory/file/stack/diagnostic fb_compat are in place; remote/display spec
  normalization plus display-host callback delegation are in place; remote
  transport and fbserv protocol behavior remain.
* Framebuffer display ownership: 39 percent.  The libimgstream/libicv/Obol
  split is defined, non-display compatibility has started, and display-capable
  fb_compat requests can delegate to a registered display host; SoBRLImageSource
  owns image-source metadata and dirty stream notifications, while
  SoBRLViewportImage and SoBRLImagePlane now consume those sources as standard
  Obol texture quads.  BRLObolWindowHost now provides the first neutral bridge
  from display framebuffer streams to Obol image nodes, and
  BRLObolHeadlessWindowHost can poll and capture those scenes through
  SoOffscreenRenderer.  QgObolWindowHost can attach the same display-host
  route to a qtcad Qt canvas, create an owned Qt toplevel QgSW host when no
  canvas exists, and consume Qt readback.  Incremental dirty-region texture
  uploads, broader dm_open routing, hardware-preferred Qt host selection, and
  Tk/MGED adapters remain.

Migration Phases:

* Phase 1, architecture freeze and guardrails: 80 percent.
* Phase 2, productionize libbrlobol: 97 percent.
* Phase 3, migrate qged/qtcad: 53 percent.
* Phase 4, migrate libged, libtclcad, and mged: 26 percent.
* Phase 5, remove lower-level BSG/DM dependencies: 52 percent.
* Phase 6, hard removal: 5 percent.


Next Push
---------

The next implementation push should make the Obol path real enough to test
against application workflows, not just isolated controller/readback pieces.

1. Define the Obol realization coverage matrix.

* Use share/db/pinewood.g and share/db/havoc.g as required hierarchy tests.
  Use share/db/faa/Generic_Twin.g as the larger mixed-primitive maturity
  candidate before the final qtcad migration gate: it is the largest in-tree
  example, includes valid and invalid BoTs, and mixes in implicit solids.
* List every primitive and database object type that the old drawing path can
  draw.
* For each type, record the intended Obol representation: native Obol shape,
  BRL-CAD Obol node, tessellated mesh, wire/vlist fallback, or deferred.
* Record the required preserved identity: db path, primitive, face, edge,
  vertex, material, visibility, selection state, and edit intent.

Completion for this step:

* A checked-in coverage table exists.
* pinewood.g and havoc.g can be loaded by the Obol realization path without
  requiring BSG scene objects.
* Unsupported types fail explicitly with diagnostics instead of silently
  disappearing.

2. Productionize libbrlobol database realization.

* Make SoBRLDatabaseSource and SoBRLSceneController responsible for real .g
  hierarchy realization and partial refresh.
* Keep traversal-relevant state in Obol fields/details, not controller side
  tables.
* Convert bg_vlist/rt_vlist wire output to Obol line/shape nodes only at the
  realization boundary.
* For large BoTs and future high-cost geometry, use the staged LoD realization
  pipeline described in the consolidated LoD notes: publish metadata, attributes/AABB,
  OBB/proxy, POP fast mesh, qualified progressive/high-quality mesh, and
  full-detail fallback as separate revision-tagged results instead of blocking
  Obol realization on a single final mesh.
  The first synchronous bridge is limited to copying already-cached
  rt_mesh_lod_info into SoBRLMeshShape metadata fields and SoBRLExportAction
  triangle records; it must not become the final staged task service or force
  POP generation in ordinary realization.
* LoD implementation sub-queue:
  1. Continue extending the RT-owned mesh-LoD facade before libbrlobol depends
     on POP residency.  Neutral current-level queries, active-data residency
     status, copied active metadata, memory shrink/reload hooks, and cache
     status/refresh/invalidate reporting are in place; richer provider
     diagnostics remain.
  2. Define provider-neutral request/result/geometry-handle types and cache
     keys that include database identity, source revision or content hash,
     object path/name, view/policy revision, draw mode, provider id/version,
     provider parameters, quality tier, bounds, primitive counts, estimated
     error when known, terminal/fallback status, and diagnostics.  Initial
     BRLObolLodRequest, BRLObolLodResult, BRLObolLodGeometryHandle, and
     deterministic cache-key helpers are in place, including typed
     dependencies, attributes, proxy payloads, and owned mesh payloads for
     staged results; the first RT mesh-cache provider task adapter is in
     place, can force a requested RT LoD level, and copies active RT mesh-LoD
     arrays before shrinking borrowed active arrays and publishing results;
     provider-specific POP mesh payload ownership and broader provider wiring
     remain.
  3. Add an owned async realization service with explicit start/stop,
     joinable workers, cancellation/generation tokens, explicit in-flight
     counts, task-graph dependencies, result drain on the UI thread, and one
     serialized bu_cache/LMDB write lane for persistent writes and stale-entry
     clears.  The initial BRLObolLodService is in place with tests for
     dependency ordering, late generation cancellation, stale result rejection,
     UI-thread result draining, cancellable debug-delay diagnostics, and
     serialized cache writes, plus owned realization-task data cleanup for
     completed and never-started queued tasks.  It now has a tested RT
     mesh-cache provider task adapter, provider-specific free helper, and
     coalesced result-ready subscription/wakeup;
     integration with persistent cache-write operations and provider-owned POP
     payloads remains.
  4. Use moodycamel::ConcurrentQueue where it fits inter-stage and result
     handoff, but keep correctness based on enqueue/dequeue success,
     cancellation/shutdown tokens, generation ids, and in-flight counts rather
     than size_approx().  size_approx() is acceptable only for diagnostics,
     idle waits, and settled checks.  The first service uses standard
     mutex/condition-variable queues as the correctness baseline; a
     concurrentqueue substitution can be evaluated later without changing the
     public service contract.
  5. Implement Stage 0/1/2 outputs first: directory/dependency records,
     display attributes, AABB, and OBB/proxy.  Preserve the cache_drawing.cpp
     debug-delay idea with production-owned diagnostics so intermediate
     placeholder states can be inspected during large-model debugging.  The
     provider-neutral result payloads and constructors for these milestones
     are in place, and SoBRLLodUpdateAction can apply those records to matching
     mesh shapes after a UI-thread service drain; the service now has
     cancellable task-level debug delay controls and delayed-task diagnostics;
     the RT mesh-cache provider task is available for cached mesh results, can
     force a requested RT LoD level, can shrink active RT arrays after copying,
     and can publish owned mesh payloads; lodBotThreshold now gates when database
     BoT realization creates LoD-backed mesh nodes.  POP provider tasks,
     provider-level active-level updates, and richer provider/policy
     scheduling remain.
  6. Wire Obol/libbrlobol to consume staged results while preserving existing
     full-field SoBRLMeshShape and vlist realization for small or synchronous
     geometry.  Late results must be ignored harmlessly when their
     source/view/policy generation tokens no longer match.  SoBRLMeshShape now
     exposes staged result fields for dependencies, attributes, proxy state,
     provider status, diagnostics, and cache identity, and can reject stale
     result keys while preserving existing full mesh realization.
     SoBRLLodUpdateAction drains staged results from BRLObolLodService, matches
     them to mesh nodes by source path/name, applies accepted records and owned
     mesh payloads, and reports stale or unmatched results.  Mesh shapes can
     build deterministic request identities from their Obol fields, and the
     view controller can produce an RT view snapshot from Obol camera/viewport
     state.  The first explicit SoBRLMeshLodSubmitAction uses those identities
     and snapshots to enqueue RT mesh-cache provider tasks for matching
     LoD-backed mesh
     nodes, and BRLObolViewController now wires explicit controller-level
     submit/drain/apply calls plus retained service wakeups over its active
     scene and database source list.  It also has an auto-submit gate that
     coalesces changed view/policy/source signatures, includes lodBotThreshold
     policy input in the source signature, tracks sanitized RT view signatures
     including direct camera field changes, exposes a forced-level policy that
     is passed to SoBRLMeshLodSubmitAction, and cancels the previous active
     generation before scheduling new RT mesh-cache requests.
     SoBRLLodMeshShape now marks the first dedicated LoD-backed BoT mesh
     representation, and BoT database realization creates it only when
     lodBotThreshold is positive and the BoT face count meets/exceeds the
     threshold while retaining existing full-field mesh data.  The first
     forced-level provider policy path is wired; richer render-time level
     selection and scheduling remain.
  7. Add a LoD-backed BoT representation, preferably SoBRLLodMeshShape, plus
     SoBRLMeshLodUpdateAction or equivalent controller logic that updates the
     active RT mesh-LoD level from Obol camera/view state before rendering and
     updates private/backend geometry only when the active level changes or a
     reload is requested.  The initial SoBRLLodMeshShape subclass is in place,
     BoT database realization uses lodBotThreshold for policy-threshold
     selection, LoD submission filters out ordinary full-detail mesh shapes
     unless explicitly configured otherwise, and export now has explicit
     full-detail versus display-LoD policy with full-detail preserving
     behavior as the default for ordinary meshes.  Dedicated LoD-backed mesh
     shapes now avoid mandatory full-detail backup when a display-LoD payload
     is active, while keeping source counts and bounds stable for request
     identity.  They can use preserved source triangles for export, measure,
     and snap only when that source geometry is already resident.  Large-geometry LoD must avoid mandatory full-detail residency:
     scene nodes should retain source identity, bounds, counts, and the
     display payload only while memory policy keeps it resident, and exact
     operations should reload or stream bounded full-detail data from the .g/RT
     provider path, including chunked
     bbox/intersection-targeted reads for meshes too large to fit in memory.
     Cached coarse spatial/index sidecars generated while building POP payloads
     are a candidate optimization for pick/snap: reuse the POP pass over source
     triangles to assign faces to normalized Morton/grid leaves, store unioned
     leaf bboxes plus source face ids or ranges, and query those leaves before
     requesting bounded exact data.  Keep this optional and useful only when
     paired with a clean librt BoT partial-read interface or cached full-detail
     chunks.
     Basic forced-level update is wired; richer view-policy level selection
     and source-backed detail access remain.
  8. Move POP generation behind the neutral provider interface as the first
     production fast-BoT provider.  Add BoT topology qualification, mark bad
     mesh topology as POP-terminal when appropriate, and add
     progressive/high-quality providers only after the POP path is reliable.
  9. Make pick, snap, measure, and export choose display-level or full-detail
     geometry explicitly, and add tests for large-BoT realization,
     view-dependent level changes, memory shrink/reload, full-detail export,
     stale late-result rejection, serialized cache writes, and absence of BSG
     LoD headers in libbrlobol.  Provider-level database invalidation coverage
     now verifies explicit RT cache-key clearing, no-refresh cache-miss
     reporting, and refresh-enabled payload regeneration.  SoBRLExportAction
     and SoBRLMeasureAction now default to FULL_DETAIL/detail-preserving
     behavior that skips active display-LoD mesh payloads and uses preserved
     full-detail source geometry when available, while DISPLAY_LEVEL opts into
     the displayed LoD geometry.  SoBRLSnapAction defaults to DISPLAY_LEVEL
     for interactive snapping while FULL_DETAIL skips active display-LoD
     payloads and can use preserved source geometry for edit/precision
     callers.  Dedicated LoD-backed mesh tests now verify display-LoD updates
     do not retain full-detail payloads but still keep source metrics for
     request identity.  Mesh ray picking now defaults to display-level
     geometry and has an explicit full-detail policy that uses resident
     preserved source triangles when available, skipping active display-LoD
     payloads when no exact source geometry is resident.  RT/source-backed
     reload when no preserved full-detail payload is resident is wired for the
     first exact-operation paths.  Mesh nodes now report estimated display,
     preserved full-detail, and total resident mesh bytes, and can explicitly
     evict preserved full-detail arrays and active display payloads while
     retaining source counts and bounds so source-backed requests still work.
     A dedicated scene traversal action plus explicit and optional
     controller-owned budget paths can now enforce a resident mesh byte budget
     by evicting preserved detail first and source-backed display payloads
     second.  Source-backed exact measure, snap, and pick requests now carry
     source-local nearest-query bounds, snap bounds/tolerance, or ray metadata
     through LoD provider params, and the direct BoT provider can reduce snap
     and pick response payloads from those bounds/tolerance and ray hints
     while preserving compact subset source face/vertex ids and applying
     exact full-detail budgets after source-local scoped payload reduction;
     scoped misses now stay no-result fallbacks instead of whole-object
     payloads, and mixed/ambiguous/malformed/non-source-local query metadata
     stays whole-object.  Template source-query params are replaced during
     full-detail source-request conversion so stale request metadata cannot
     create duplicate or mixed query hints.  A reusable
     libbrlobol RT pick cache now covers exact implicit-solid and comb-instance
     hit identity without re-prepping the same explicit object set for every
     ray.
     Chunked/bbox-targeted detail reads for out-of-core meshes and
     remaining tool integration/refinement of librt-backed exact CSG/comb
     picks remain.
     Evaluate a coarse POP-build spatial sidecar plus selective BoT read or
     cached full-detail chunk API for exact pick/snap subsets before committing
     to that complexity.
* Add tests for draw, erase, partial refresh, selection identity, path
  identity, bbox/autoview data, and unsupported-object diagnostics.

Completion for this step:

* libbrlobol can realize, update, inspect, select, and render the required
  model set without libbsg/libdm scene objects.
* Rebuilding a controller from an existing Obol scene does not lose
  authoritative state.
* Staged realization results can arrive asynchronously, apply only to matching
  source/view/policy revisions, and leave stale late results harmless.

Completion for this step:

  title/status display now reads
  center, size, AET, perspective, and scale through the RT legacy adapter;
  libtclcad view draw matrix/perspective, wrapper perspective reads, command
  center/scale reads, axes size scaling, polygon command view snapshots, and
  qged view-info numeric display reads also go through the RT legacy
  adapter; qtcad Obol camera sync, snap tolerance, input scaling, sketch
  transform, selection ray/rectangle view snapshots, 2D measurement
  view-to-model conversion, quad-view source unit/size copies, and SW/GL
  fallback framebuffer-size or matrix-load reads plus qtcad test/demo view
  setup writes now use the same RT view adapter boundary, and qtcad quad-view
  visible-path propagation now uses
  Obol export identity in display-level mode instead of direct BSG export
  queries; qtcad selection's public header now exposes selected paths without
  naming legacy BSG pick-result objects while keeping the remaining fallback
  internal; qtcad GED draw observer sync and real-model Obol
  draw/readback workflows now keep controller-owned database sources realized
  after delayed Obol field sensors drain; libtclcad display-bound setup, MGED ADC
  view-unit math, the shared GED2PM1 conversion, MGED axes overlay scaling,
  MGED attach window bounds, MGED view-scale clamping, and MGED menu/faceplate
  drawing plus slider/pen normalized-coordinate handling now use the same RT
  view numeric boundary, and MGED event/generic display-manager mouse and ADC
  numeric paths no longer use BSG-owned view-coordinate constants.  Sketch
  conversion now uses opaque rt_sketch_polygon in the neutral header
  and RT-owned implementation, with BSG polygon refs limited to the explicit
  legacy adapter header and implementation unit.  rt_mesh_lod loading/data
  access, copied active-state metadata, cache status, reported refresh, and
  explicit invalidation are available through RT-owned helpers.  The libged
  draw BoT LoD path also obtains cache status, refreshes missing or stale
  entries, owns the returned rt_mesh_lod handle, publishes active geometry
  through rt_mesh_lod_data_get, and passes that RT handle to private GED
  adapters for the remaining transitional BSG scene-ref visibility/load behavior.
  Generated BRep mesh payloads are stored with db_mesh_lod_store_mesh, and
  BRep draw realization now owns an rt_mesh_lod handle before borrowing the
  BSG payload through the RT legacy adapter for transitional scene-ref load
  behavior; the full-detail BRep
  BSG callback registration and payload field mutation are isolated in
  bsg_ged_draw_brep.cpp instead of draw.cpp.  Direct BSG cache mutation is
  confined to the RT cache implementation; BRep tessellation remains the documented
  transitional libged-side producer.  BSG mesh-LoD unwrapping remains private
  to librt implementation units, with private GED adapters passing RT-handle
  parameters through the RT legacy BSG view/scene-ref boundary.
  rt_view_info adapter,
  sanitize, RT view
  metric helper, and mesh-LoD helper behavior are covered by the BSG-free
  src/librt/tests/view_info.c; src/librt/tests/view_legacy_bsg.c covers the
  transitional BSG adapter; rt_lod validates cached LoD levels through the
  neutral helper surface.  libged database-index LoD cache invalidation uses
  db_mesh_lod_invalidate instead of direct BSG key/cache APIs, and view lod
  cache clearing uses db_mesh_lod_clear/rt_mesh_lod_cache_clear_all instead of
  direct BSG cache-clear calls.  View lod BoT cache refresh and cache-exists
  checks use db_mesh_lod_refresh/status rather than direct BSG key lookup; the
  draw BoT mesh-LoD path uses db_mesh_lod_status/refresh/get and RT handle
  ownership instead of direct BSG key/cache generation, while generated BRep
  mesh payloads are written through db_mesh_lod_store_mesh instead of direct
  BSG cache APIs.  The remaining BRep tessellation producers in view lod and
  draw are explicitly transitional.  The draw source state and tree-walk
  draw_data_t no longer carry bsg_mesh_lod_context or ged_lod through to
  realization, ged_draw_source_state no longer stores a borrowed bsg_mesh_lod
  pointer, and public struct ged no longer exposes or manually manages a
  BSG mesh-LoD context.  General BSG draw helper units without mesh-LoD
  payload operations, plus the GED draw tests that now use RT cache helpers,
  no longer include bsg/lod.h; draw.cpp routes BRep mesh-LoD full-detail
  setup through the private BREP draw helper, and that helper now registers
  neutral RT mesh-LoD detail callbacks rather than BSG callback signatures.
  Draw-ref reset calls the private scene-ref LoD free
  adapter rather than direct BSG LoD APIs.  The private libged BSG-to-RT view
  adapter now uses only RT legacy helpers for BSG LoD bounds callbacks,
  view scale, and scene-ref LoD load/free.  Draw tests check the transaction
  bounds-update hook through the private helper rather than direct
  bsg_view_bounds comparisons.  qtcad quad views, MGED zoom/idle/attach paths,
  and libtclcad commands/wrapper redraw checks copy, inspect, and apply LoD
  policy through rt/view_legacy_bsg.h instead of direct BSG policy structs or
  get/set calls.  All are enforced by the pivot guard.
* The initial provider-neutral LoD request, result, cache-key, and
  geometry-handle types are in place before wiring libbrlobol to POP.  Keep
  POP as the first production fast BoT provider behind RT/provider APIs; add
  BoT topology qualification and progressive-mesh generation later as separate
  providers.  Result records carry object path, source revision, view/policy
  revision, result kind, quality tier, provider id, cache key or geometry
  handle, bounds, primitive counts, estimated error when known,
  terminal/fallback status, and diagnostics.  The current SoBRLMeshShape and
  SoBRLExportAction LoD summary fields are only the first visible metadata
  slice of that result contract.  The initial BRLObolLodService provides the
  staged worker, generation cancellation, dependency, result-drain, and
  serialized cache-write lane needed by future providers.  Stage 0/1/2 result
  payloads now carry dependency records, display attributes, AABB milestones,
  and proxy/OBB records.  Staged mesh results can also carry owned
  indexed-triangle payloads copied from RT mesh-LoD data.  SoBRLMeshShape
  consumes those staged records into Obol fields, applies owned mesh payloads
  through its existing geometry fields, and rejects stale, cancelled, error,
  and cache-miss result statuses.  SoBRLLodUpdateAction drains service results
  and applies accepted staged records to matching mesh nodes.  Mesh shapes can
  build deterministic request identities from their Obol fields, and
  BRLObolViewController can produce sanitized rt_view_info snapshots from its
  Obol camera/viewport state.  The first explicit SoBRLMeshLodSubmitAction
  uses those identities and snapshots to enqueue RT mesh-cache provider tasks
  for matching LoD-backed mesh nodes and lets BRLObolLodService own provider
  task cleanup after completion,
  cancellation, or shutdown before execution.  BRLObolViewController now has
  an explicit controller-facing submit/drain/apply path over database sources
  so applications can exercise the staged path without hand-instantiating the
  actions, plus a retained result-ready subscription that turns worker queue
  publication into a main-thread pending-result drain before render.  It can
  also coalesce automatic submissions by changed view/policy/source signature
  before render.  The first RT
  mesh-cache provider task can refresh/load cached RT mesh LoD through the
  service and uses the RT active-data residency query to keep unloaded active
  levels on the cache-miss result path.  Dedicated LoD-backed Obol mesh nodes
  now opt out of preserving full-detail arrays when display-LoD payloads are
  applied and retain lightweight source metrics for stable request keys, but
  the service does not yet own POP payload residency, source-backed
  full-detail fallback, chunked/bounded
  detail fetches for exact operations, optional acceleration/index sidecars
  for targeted triangle subset queries, memory-budget eviction policy, richer
  provider/policy scheduling, or update dedicated LoD-backed Obol mesh nodes.
* Keep the default plot3 implementation in its normal libbg implementation
  unit.
* Keep standalone plot3 utility links on libbg/libbu instead of libbsg, except
  for explicit display/Tk compatibility utilities such as plot3-dm.
* Keep bg_line_layer as the neutral in-memory diagnostic container.  The
  bg_line_layer-to-plot3 export helpers are available as a simple diagnostic
  output path that does not require Obol execution.
* Keep bsg/plot3.h only as a compatibility wrapper until libbsg removal.
* Do not reintroduce old BSG TIG or vectfont public headers.
* Move useful framebuffer/image-streaming behavior out of libdm according to
  the framebuffer plan: libimgstream for mutable streams, transport, producers,
  subscribers, fan-out, fbserv compatibility, and fb_* wrappers; libicv for
  static image/codecs/lookup-table operations; Obol/libbrlobol for display,
  texture nodes, readback, image placement, and window/input hosting.
* Preserve useful legacy transport and protocol workflows through
  libimgstream compatibility while replacing display-capable framebuffer
  backends with Obol image display.

Completion for this step:

* Low-level libraries no longer need BSG names for vlist, line-layer, plot,
  or diagnostic containers.
* Remaining libdm framebuffer use is either deleted, routed through
  libimgstream/fb_compat, replaced by Obol readback/display, or assigned to
  libicv image-processing utilities.

7. Continue the libimgstream migration batch.

* The libimgstream build target and initial opaque C API are in place.
* The first in-memory RGB/RGBA stream layer implements imgstream_get_info,
  resize, clear, full frame read/write, rect read/write, dirty rectangle
  generation, subscriber callbacks, producer begin/end, and
  imgstream_snapshot_icv conversion.
* Keep expanding focused tests for stream lifecycle, resize behavior, pixel
  operations, dirty-update generation, subscriber notification, and snapshot
  conversion as compatibility callers arrive.
* Add the remaining metadata/state APIs needed by framebuffer compatibility
  before routing old fb state through libimgstream.
* The first fb compatibility wrapper entry points are in place for memory
  stream open/close, clear, read/write, RGB readrect/writerect,
  BW readrect/writerect, view/getview, window, zoom, cursor/getcursor,
  scursor, setcursor, reset, viewport, and no-op non-display poll/poll-rate.
* Raw RGB file compatibility is in place for ordinary file paths and explicit
  /dev/disk:<path> or /dev/disk=<path> spelling.  File-backed streams load
  existing exact-sized pixel data, expose the same imgstream operations, and
  flush raw RGB data on request or close.
* /dev/stack fan-out compatibility is in place for semicolon-separated
  memory/file child sinks.  Writes, rectangle writes, clear, view, cursor,
  poll, and flush propagate to every child; reads use the first child, matching
  the legacy stack framebuffer behavior.
* /dev/null, /dev/debug, and /dev/txt diagnostic sink compatibility is in
  place.  These sinks expose dimensions and preserved view/cursor state,
  discard writes without mutating the backing stream, return zero pixels for
  reads, and keep /dev/debug logging behavior for operation tracing.
* Neutral colormap compatibility is in place.  imgstream_fb_colormap stores the
  legacy 256-entry RGB transfer table with 16-bit channels, rmap/wmap preserve
  per-stream state, and wmap(NULL) resets to the default linear map.
* Remote/display framebuffer spec normalization is in place.  The
  imgstream_fb_spec_info helper records parsed file/fan-out targets,
  remote TCP or IPC targets, host/port/device fields, display kind, and
  diagnostic kind for legacy spelling such as numeric ports, host:port,
  host:port:device, tcp:, ipc:, and /dev/qtgl.
* Display-host callback registration is in place.  Display-capable
  framebuffer opens create an RGB stream and delegate open/close, view,
  cursor, screen-cursor, cursor-shape, viewport, reset, flush, poll, and
  poll-rate operations to the registered display host.  If no host is
  registered, display specifiers still fail open explicitly.
* Add remote transport, fbserv protocol, and real Obol/libbrlobol display-host
  implementations in later batches.

Completion for this step:

* A non-display image stream can be created, updated, observed, read back, and
  converted to/from libicv data without linking libdm.
* The initial API remains small, opaque, and uses imgstream_* names.
* Memory framebuffer compatibility requests route to libimgstream-owned RGB
  buffers, file compatibility requests route to libimgstream-backed raw RGB
  file sinks/sources, and /dev/stack fan-out compatibility routes to supported
  child sinks.  Diagnostic framebuffer compatibility routes to non-display
  diagnostic sinks.  Legacy colormap state is preserved through neutral
  libimgstream types.  Remote and other old framebuffer specifiers are parsed
  for future migration and rejected explicitly for now; display framebuffer
  specifiers open only when a toolkit-neutral display host is registered.
* No Obol display code depends on libimgstream until the stream layer is
  independently tested.

8. Extract framebuffer compatibility and display ownership.

* Move useful /dev/mem behavior to core libimgstream in-memory streams.
* Move file sink/source and colormap image transforms to libicv/libimgstream
  as appropriate.
* Move /dev/remote, host:port, ipc:<addr>, PKG_ADDR, FBSERV_TOKEN,
  FBSERV_TLS, and MSG_FB* behavior to libimgstream transport/fb_compat.
* Preserve legacy fb_view, fb_getview, fb_window, fb_zoom, cursor, poll,
  expose/resize, linger, and simple mouse behavior as compatibility/display
  state.
* SoBRLImageSource is in place for static icv_image_t data and borrowed live
  imgstream sources, including stream metadata fields and dirty-region
  subscription updates.  SoBRLViewportImage and SoBRLImagePlane are in place as
  standard Obol texture-quad consumers driven by source data/dirty revisions;
  replace full rebuilds with dirty-region backend texture uploads and connect
  the display-host/window-host paths.
* The neutral BRLObolWindowHost contract and imgstream display-host
  registration are in place for display-capable fb_open.  The first
  headless/offscreen BRLObolHeadlessWindowHost adapter can drain render
  requests and capture pixels through SoOffscreenRenderer.  qtcad's
  QgObolWindowHost can attach a Qt canvas to the same display-host path and
  can create an owned visible Qt toplevel QgSW host for display-capable
  framebuffer requests when no canvas exists.  Connect dm_open/display-host
  routing through that contract, replace the temporary owned-window fallback
  with final qtcad/qged host policy, and add the Tk/MGED adapter.

Completion for this step:

* fbserv, rt -F, remrt, and representative src/fb tools can run through
  libimgstream compatibility while display-capable requests are shown by an
  Obol/libbrlobol display host or fail explicitly when no host is registered.
* Old framebuffer backend identities no longer define the implementation
  architecture.


Migration Phases
----------------

Phase 1: Architecture freeze and guardrails

* Document Obol as BRL-CAD's canonical drawing API.
* Keep brlobol_pivot_guard active in transitional mode.
* Inventory all current BSG/DM dependencies.
* Convert the guard to strict mode before hard removal.

Done when:

* The policy is documented.
* New Obol-canonical directories are clean of BSG/DM dependencies.
* Legacy violations are inventoried and assigned to later phases.

Phase 2: Productionize libbrlobol

* Complete primitive and hierarchy realization.
* Add staged provider-neutral LoD realization for large BoTs, starting with
  POP behind the RT mesh-LoD facade and preserving full-detail fallback for
  exact operations.
* Preserve identity, material, visibility, selection, draw intent, edit
  preview, and diagnostics on Obol nodes/fields/details.
* Implement snap, measure, pick, export, partial refresh, HUD, overlays,
  faceplate, grid, axes, ADC, and text behavior with Obol concepts.

Done when:

* libbrlobol can load, realize, inspect, select, pick, snap, measure, export,
  and render the agreed model set without libbsg/libdm.
* Large BoTs can render through LoD-backed Obol geometry, refine from view
  changes, and still support explicit full-detail pick/measure/snap/export
  policies.
* Controller rebuild from an existing Obol scene works without lost state.
* libbrlobol has no generic BSG replacement API.

Phase 3: Migrate qged/qtcad

* Replace visible qtcad rendering with Obol rendering.
* Move qged open/draw/erase/view/autoview/navigation/pick/select/snap/
  measure/edit-preview/HUD/screenshot workflows to Obol.
* Use Obol software/offscreen fallback paths instead of libdm swrast.

Done when:

* qged/qtcad render real models through Obol only.
* qged/qtcad support the expected interactive workflows through Obol.
* qged/qtcad no longer link libbsg/libdm or depend on dm_plugins.

Phase 4: Migrate libged, libtclcad, and mged

* Replace bsg_ged_draw* with an Obol-backed GED scene service.
* Port TclCAD view creation, input handling, refresh, faceplate, and readback
  to the Obol controller bridge.
* Keep mged supported with a proper Tcl/Tk Obol widget for performant native
  rendering when system graphics APIs are available.  Obol offscreen rendering
  plus Tk image presentation is a fallback path, not the target design.
* Route display-window creation through the libbrlobol window host contract
  rather than preserving dm_open as a public display-manager abstraction.

Done when:

* libged and libtclcad no longer link libbsg/libdm.
* mged core display workflows pass scripted regression tests using Obol.
* No GED/TclCAD command requires a public bsg_view or dm object.

Phase 5: Remove lower-level BSG/DM dependencies

* Finish moving neutral geometry/debug/export containers to libbg/librt names.
* Retire BSG vlist and line-layer wrappers.
* Replace scene text and labels with Obol text.
* Grow libimgstream from the initial memory-stream target into the owner of
  useful framebuffer storage, streaming, producer/subscriber, transport,
  fbserv, and fb compatibility behavior.
* Move static image/codecs/lookup-table behavior to libicv.
* Replace libdm framebuffer display behavior with Obol image nodes and
  libbrlobol display-host/window-host paths.

Done when:

* No non-legacy target in src/source_dirs.cmake depends on libbsg/libdm.
* Low-level libraries do not include BSG/DM headers for scene or display
  behavior.
* Framebuffer tools use libimgstream/fb_compat, libicv, or Obol readback and
  display paths rather than libdm framebuffer backends.

Phase 6: Hard removal

* Delete installed BSG and DM public headers.
* Delete libbsg, libdm, and dm_plugins from build and install.
* Remove temporary allowlists and transitional bridges.
* Remove obsolete fb_* compatibility wrappers only after rt, remrt, qged,
  TclCAD/mged, and required tools use libimgstream or Obol directly.  Keep
  transport and MSG_FB* compatibility where it remains useful independent of
  the old libdm framebuffer backend API.
* Update documentation, examples, man pages, package metadata, pkg-config
  files, and build files.
* Enable strict dependency checks.

Done when:

* Full build/install produces no libbsg, no libdm, no dm_plugins, and no
  public BSG/DM headers.
* No source target links libbsg/libdm.
* No source code includes BSG/DM headers outside intentionally archived
  historical documentation.
* Obol is documented as the canonical BRL-CAD scene API.


Consolidated Historical Notes
-----------------------------

This section preserves the useful context from the former root notes that were
not meant to stay as separate active handoffs.

newdraw branch review:

* The newdraw branch was a large architectural migration, not a cosmetic
  libbv rename.  The review snapshot measured roughly 1050 changed files,
  155298 insertions, and 42636 deletions.
* The strongest value in newdraw was centralized semantic drawing state:
  libged owned draw records, draw transactions, database indexes, selection
  state, stale tracking, and shared frontend semantics; libdm gained a backend
  render-item/resource-cache boundary; MGED, TclCAD, and qged moved toward one
  common draw-state model.
* The main risk was scope and public API stability.  libbsg was broader than
  libbv: scene graph, view state, render items, payloads, selection, snapping,
  overlays, HUD, LoD, and backend adapters.  The Obol migration keeps the
  useful semantic lessons but avoids preserving libbsg as the canonical public
  drawing API.
* The review's validation lesson still applies: make the risk bounded with
  main-compatible behavioral tests, unchanged tests on the migrated path,
  Obol/newdraw-only semantic tests, stress/scale/performance gates, and an
  explicit evidence package before broad legacy removal.

Modern drawing/model handoff:

* The database remains authoritative.  Draw records, selections, edit
  previews, Qt models, indexes, and application state are derived and
  rebuildable.
* Native GED services are the desired ownership model:
  GedDbIndex owns object/path/reverse-use/affected-path indexing;
  GedEventTxn owns mutation batching, event coalescing, reconcile phases, and
  post-reconcile observer notification; GedDrawState owns retained draw
  intents, typed realized geometry, stale state, draw/erase/clear/redraw/who
  behavior, and shared/independent view transactions; GedSelectionState owns
  semantic selection and highlight synchronization.
* Do not recreate DbiState, BViewState, gedp->dbi_state,
  ged_dbi_state_*, include/ged/dbi_state.h, src/libged/dbi.h, or
  src/libged/dbi_state.cpp as the ownership model.
* Modern drawing publication is typed: line sets, point sets, indexed line
  sets, indexed face sets, text, annotations, images, overlays, proxies, and
  edit previews.  Modern paths should not expose raw vlists/vlblocks or raw
  BSG internals.
* Observers should see stable post-reconcile state only.  Avoid notifying UI
  or application consumers while database, draw, selection, or edit state is
  partially mutated.
* Scale work should be bounded by affected objects, active draw intents,
  expanded Qt branches, selected/edit targets, and active views.  Full rebuilds
  are acceptable for open-file, explicit rebuild, corruption, batch import, or
  events whose scope cannot yet be localized.
* Avoid full path-string scans in hot update paths.  Prefer path hashes,
  reverse-use answers, affected-path hints, and loaded-row indexes.  If full
  path matching is unavoidable, benchmark it at scale and add a regression
  gate.

Obol prototype lessons:

* The prototype proved enough to proceed with Obol-canonical work: database
  source nodes, SoBRLRealizeAction, real librt/db_walk_tree traversal,
  combination instance transforms, SoBRLVListShape, SoBRLMeshShape, shaded
  ARB8/RPP/BoT realization, NMG tessellation fallback, pick details, snap,
  measure, export, draw-intent fields, edit preview, field-sensor
  invalidation, partial source refresh, scene-controller rebuild from existing
  Obol nodes, grid/axes/ADC overlays, direct Obol GL rendering, non-GL
  renderScene proof, NanoRT proof, readback, and Obol HUD use without libdm.
* Every new feature should first be expressed as normal Obol scene
  programming: node fields, field sensors/notification, actions, details,
  state/elements only where traversal state is genuinely needed, and
  application-owned backends through Obol's context/backend interface.
* Do not add a generic BRL-CAD node/field/action/render layer unless a
  concrete test shows Obol cannot represent the needed behavior cleanly.
* Continue the Obol pivot if libbrlobol remains a small BRL-CAD extension
  layer on top of Obol.  Pause and reconsider if libbrlobol starts exposing
  generic scene, field, render-item, or backend-adapter types that duplicate
  Obol.

LoD pipeline details:

* The source database is authoritative.  LoD data is disposable derived
  display/cache data.
* The staged service should produce metadata/dependency records, attributes
  and AABB, OBB or cheap proxy, fast display LoD, qualified high-quality LoD,
  and exact/full-detail fallback as separate revision-tagged results.
* Provider-neutral request keys should include database identity, source
  revision or content hash, object path/name, view/policy revision, draw mode,
  provider id/version, provider parameters, quality tier, bounds, primitive
  counts, estimated error where known, terminal/fallback status, and
  diagnostics.
* POP Buffer generation is the first production fast-BoT provider because it
  is robust for triangle soup and invalid BoTs.  Progressive mesh generation
  should be a later provider for qualified clean topology.
* The older cache_drawing.cpp experiment contributed useful shape: staged
  fast-to-slow workers, main-thread result queue, serialized cache writer,
  debug delay knobs, and AABB/OBB/LoD milestones.  Do not carry forward its
  detached workers, object-name-only keys, void* lifetime, fixed linear
  assumptions, or informal rt_db_internal ownership.
* concurrentqueue.h may be used for high-volume producer/consumer handoff if
  it improves implementation quality, but correctness must come from
  enqueue/dequeue success, cancellation/shutdown tokens, generation ids, and
  in-flight counts.  size_approx() is only diagnostic.
* Result-ready wakeups are signals only.  Workers publish BRLObolLodResult
  records and coalesced wakeups; the owning view/controller drains results on
  the UI/main thread and applies SoBRLLodUpdateAction to matching scene nodes.
* Large LoD-backed BoTs must avoid mandatory full-detail residency.  Exact
  operations should request source-backed whole-object reloads, bounded
  chunks, or bbox/intersection-targeted subsets through the RT/provider path.
* A POP-build spatial sidecar is plausible and should remain optional: while
  walking source triangles, assign faces to coarse normalized Morton/grid
  buckets, union leaf bboxes, store source face ids or ranges, and build a
  shallow tree.  It is useful only if paired with a BoT partial-read API or
  cached full-detail chunk provider.

Framebuffer and image-stream plan:

* Obol owns display, placement, layering, texture upload, readback, backend
  selection, window hosting, and input integration.
* libimgstream owns mutable streams, pixel storage, dimensions, dirty
  rectangles, generation counters, clear/read/write/read-rect/write-rect,
  snapshots, producers, subscribers, fan-out, in-memory streams, file
  sinks/sources, transport, fb compatibility, null/debug/txt diagnostics, and
  fbserv/remrt/rt workflows during migration.
* libicv owns static image containers, codecs, file I/O, image transforms, and
  neutral colormap/lookup-table operations.
* fb compatibility lives inside libimgstream as fb_compat.h/fb_compat.c unless
  implementation evidence justifies a split.
* Preserve useful transport mechanisms: host:port, ipc:<addr>, PKG_ADDR
  handoff, FBSERV_TOKEN, FBSERV_TLS, and MSG_FB* where needed.
* Treat legacy view, cursor, poll, expose/resize, linger, simple mouse, and
  pan/zoom behavior as compatibility/display state.  Do not let it define the
  core image-stream API.
* Device spelling maps under compatibility: /dev/mem becomes in-memory stream
  behavior; /dev/disk becomes file sink/source behavior; /dev/stack becomes
  fan-out; /dev/null, /dev/debug, and /dev/txt become diagnostic sinks;
  remote, host:port, tcp:, and ipc: spellings become transport endpoints;
  display specifiers such as /dev/qtgl, /dev/ogl, /dev/wgl, /dev/X, and
  /dev/swrast request display through an active Obol/libbrlobol host or fail
  explicitly when no host exists.
* The libbrlobol window-host contract should hide toolkit details.  Qt, Tk,
  GLX/WGL, and other native handles belong in adapter-private code or explicit
  application integration hooks.  Long-term input bindings should be semantic,
  not Tcl strings, although the Tk adapter may translate legacy Tcl bindings
  during migration.


Verification Baseline
---------------------

Use this short baseline after doc-only or small migration changes:

* ninja -C build
* ctest --test-dir build -R
  '^(brlobol_pivot_guard|rt_view_info|bg_vlist_basic|bg_line_layer_basic|libbsg_feature|libbsg_api_hygiene|libbsg_public_headers|ged_test_gqa|ged_test_cmd_schemas|ged_test_ged_draw_scene|regress-gchecker|regress-gqa)$'
  --output-on-failure
* git diff --check
* rg -n '^<<<<<<< |^=======$|^>>>>>>> |bsg/tig\.h|bsg/vectfont\.h' include
  src obol_activework.txt obol_worklog.txt CMakeLists.txt misc/CMake

Use broader testing before removing a legacy path:

* full ctest --test-dir build --output-on-failure
* qged/qtcad workflow tests against pinewood.g, havoc.g, and
  share/db/faa/Generic_Twin.g once the Obol path is mature enough for the
  larger mixed implicit/BoT model.
* Obol render backend tests for Qt GL, offscreen/OSMesa, and NanoRT/software
  paths as applicable.
* libimgstream tests for stream lifecycle, resize, pixel operations,
  dirty-region updates, producer/subscriber lifecycle, icv snapshots,
  transport, and framebuffer protocol compatibility as applicable.
* Dependency checks proving the removed BSG/DM path is no longer installed,
  linked, or included.


Performance Gates
-----------------

Before final BSG/DM removal:

* Initial realization/render on selected regression models should be within
  25 percent of the current path, or have an explicit documented and accepted
  reason.
* Camera-only redraw and selection/pick updates should be equal or faster
  than the old path on the selected regression models.
* Small imports and small hierarchy updates must not show pathological
  scaling.
* regress-gqa should remain a sub-second test unless the test scope is
  deliberately expanded.


Open Questions
--------------

* Which Obol upstream APIs need to be added or improved once qtcad uses Obol
  as the visible renderer?  This needs more implementation evidence and
  discussion before turning into concrete migration tasks.

Resolved decisions:

* plot3 should gain a normal libbg implementation unit; plot3.h is unlikely to
  be retired.
* bg_line_layer-to-plot3 export should remain available as a simple diagnostic
  output path for debugging without requiring Obol execution.
* MGED should target a proper Tcl/Tk Obol widget for performant native
  rendering when system OpenGL/Vulkan/etc. are available; OSMesa/offscreen
  rendering plus image presentation remains a slower fallback.
* Residual libdm framebuffer behavior should split as described in
  the framebuffer plan: libimgstream for mutable streams, producer/subscriber state,
  transport, fbserv behavior, and fb compatibility; libicv for static images,
  codecs, and lookup-table image transforms; Obol/libbrlobol for image
  display, texture nodes, readback, display-host callbacks, and window/input
  hosting.


Final Completion Criteria
-------------------------

The migration is complete when:

* Obol is the only scene/rendering API used by BRL-CAD interactive
  applications.
* qged/qtcad and mged pass their Obol display workflows.
* libbsg, libdm, and dm_plugins are absent from build, install, public
  headers, pkg-config files, and linked target graphs.
* libbrlobol remains BRL-specific and does not contain a generic BSG
  replacement.
* Documentation names Obol as the canonical scene API and explains where
  BRL-CAD Obol extensions live.


2026-06-21: libged view feature adapter slices
----------------------------------------------

Moved the `view obj ... line` command's direct BSG feature-overlay operations
behind the private GED draw view adapter.  `src/libged/view/lines.c` now asks
the adapter to test line-feature existence, create persistent model annotation
line features, and append points.  The BSG-backed implementation remains in
`src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_view.c`, which keeps the transitional feature,
geometry, and overlay calls at the draw bridge boundary.

The follow-up batches moved label creation, data-axes state, and Tcl data-line
overlay state through the same boundary.  `src/libged/view/labels.c` now uses
the adapter for label feature existence and creation/data replacement.
`src/libged/view/axes.c` now uses a neutral GED draw axes-state record for
data-axes creation, position, size, line width, and color updates while the
bridge maps those fields to the temporary BSG axes payload.  `view data_lines`
and `view sdata_lines` now use the adapter for feature visibility/removal,
style reads/writes, point copying, and paired line replacement while retaining
their existing RT snap-line adapter calls.  The gobject overlay create/remove
path now asks the same adapter to test feature existence, create the temporary
overlay scene ref, and remove the overlay feature, leaving the wireframe
gathering and draw-shape fullpath state unchanged for this slice.  The main
`view obj remove` feature path now also uses the adapter for existence and
removal, so ordinary removal of those temporary overlay features no longer
calls `bsg_feature_remove` directly from `src/libged/view/objs.cpp`.  The
generic view-object draw/color/arrow/update paths now use a neutral
`ged_draw_view_feature_style` record plus adapter style-get, style-apply, and
feature-realize helpers, keeping `bsg_feature_ref`, `struct bsg_feature_style`,
and `bsg_feature_realize` inside `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_view.c`.
The top-level `view vZ` feature-depth calculation now uses named and
iterating feature-depth helpers from the same private adapter, so
`src/libged/view/view.c` no longer includes `bsg/feature.h` or calls
`bsg_feature_find`, `bsg_feature_view_depth`, or `bsg_feature_visit` directly
for that deprecated calculation path.
The `view selections` count path now uses `ged_draw_view_selection_count`,
leaving the temporary BSG selection count lookup inside
`src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_view.c`.
Faceplate command dispatch now calls `ged_draw_view_hud_sync`, leaving the
temporary BSG HUD synchronization call inside the same private view adapter.
The `view snap` grid and line snap paths now call
`ged_draw_view_snap_first_candidate`, leaving temporary BSG snap-kind mapping,
candidate lookup, result storage, and result cleanup inside the same private
view adapter.
The `view obj ... polygon` command path now uses neutral
`ged_draw_view_polygon_ref` handles and `ged_draw_view_polygon_record` state
for polygon creation, point editing, contour open/close, area/overlap
queries, fill and fill-color updates, sketch import/export, and CSG.  The
temporary `bsg_polygon_ref` storage, polygon record/data reads,
`bsg_polygon_*` mutations, BSG view-polygon lookups, and RT sketch legacy BSG
conversion calls are now confined to `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_view.c`.
The same adapter now also has a neutral `ged_draw_view_lines_replace` helper
for named diagnostic line overlays with optional move/draw command streams.
`src/libged/joint/joint.c`, `src/libged/bot/bot_fuse.c`,
`src/libged/bot/check.cpp`, `src/libged/bot/bot.cpp`,
`src/libged/lint/lint.cpp`, `src/libged/gdiff/gdiff.c`, and
`src/libged/draw/preview.cpp` use that helper and the neutral feature-style
record instead of directly replacing, creating, coloring, or removing BSG line
features.  The lint and preview/bot face-plot paths now carry their existing
move/draw command streams through the adapter using neutral `BG_GEOMETRY_*`
line commands rather than forcing BSG geometry tokens to stay in the command
files.
The labelvert/labelface producers in `src/libged/illum/illum.c` and
`src/libged/nmg/nmg.c` now build neutral `ged_draw_view_label_data` arrays and
publish them through `ged_draw_view_context_labels_replace`; BSG label-data
structs, label feature creation, label replacement, and feature removal stay
inside the private GED draw view adapter.
The adapter now also exposes `ged_draw_view_line_layer_builder_replace` for
the retained BSG fallback used when no Obol diagnostic line-layer handler is
registered.  `src/libged/check/check_overlaps.c`,
`src/libged/gqa/gqa.cpp`, `src/libged/draw/draw.c`, and
`src/libged/vutil.c` now publish through `ged_diagnostic_line_layer_publish`
first and use that adapter helper for the fallback, so those files no longer
include `bsg/feature.h` or cast `bg_line_layer_builder` to BSG line-layer
builder types.  `src/libged/brep/plot.cpp` now uses the context-form
line-layer fallback adapter, and its separate indexed face-set publishing path
later moved through the same private context adapter boundary.
The same adapter now also exposes `ged_draw_view_line_layers_replace` for
named diagnostic line-layer arrays.  `src/libged/ged_util.cpp` uses
`ged_draw_view_line_layer_data` plus `BG_GEOMETRY_*` command constants for
uplot/qray/rtcheck/overlay feature publishing, removing direct
`bsg/feature.h`, `bsg/geometry.h`, `bsg_feature_line_layer`, and
`bsg_feature_replace_line_layers` use from the uplot parser/publisher.
The adapter now also owns prefix feature cleanup through
`ged_draw_view_features_remove_prefix`.  `src/libged/nirt/nirt.cpp` uses the
single-feature remove adapter for stale qray output cleanup, and
`src/libged/rtcheck/rtcheck2.cpp` uses the prefix cleanup adapter before
republishing rtcheck uplot output, removing direct feature remove, visit, and
record traversal from both command files.
The BRep indexed face-set publishing path now uses
`ged_draw_view_indexed_face_set_replace` and the neutral feature-style record,
so `src/libged/brep/plot.cpp` no longer includes `bsg/feature.h` or calls
`bsg_feature_replace_indexed_face_set` directly.
The evaluated-wireframe line-set builders in `src/libged/draw/bigE.c` and
`src/libged/wireframe_eval.c` now use `bg/line_layer.h` and
`BG_GEOMETRY_LINE_*` command tokens instead of including `bsg/geometry.h` for
line move/draw command values.
The typed overlay insertion record now uses a GED-private
`ged_draw_overlay_geometry_kind` enum rather than exposing
`bsg_geometry_node_kind`.  `src/libged/vdraw/vdraw.c` now fills that neutral
kind and uses `BG_GEOMETRY_LINE_*` commands for line overlays; the conversion
to BSG geometry node kinds remains in `src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_overlay.c`.
The mesh-LoD indexed-face update path in `src/libged/draw.cpp` now calls
`ged_draw_scene_ref_update_indexed_face_set`, leaving the temporary
`bsg_geometry_ref_update_indexed_face_set` operation inside
`src/libged/bsg_ged_draw_source.c`.  The now-unused raw
`ged_draw_scene_ref_geometry_ref` accessor was removed from the private draw
bridge surface.

The pivot guard now requires `src/libged/view/lines.c`,
`src/libged/view/labels.c`, `src/libged/view/axes.c`,
`src/libged/view/data_lines.c`, and `src/libged/view/gobjs.cpp` to use those
adapter helpers and rejects direct `bsg_feature_*`, `BSG_FEATURE_*`,
`BSG_GEOMETRY_*`, `BSG_OVERLAY_*`, and `struct bsg_axes` usage in the migrated
command files.  It also rejects direct BSG feature API use in
`src/libged/view/objs.cpp` and the `view vZ` implementation in
`src/libged/view/view.c`, plus direct BSG selection count reads in the same
view command file, direct BSG snap-result handling in the snap command file,
direct BSG HUD sync in the faceplate command file, and the migrated
diagnostic line-overlay producers.
It also rejects direct BSG feature API use in the migrated labelvert/labelface
label producers and the migrated diagnostic line-layer fallback producers.
It also rejects direct BSG feature or geometry use in the migrated uplot
line-layer feature publisher, and direct BSG feature cleanup use in the qray
and rtcheck cleanup paths.  It also rejects direct BSG feature use in the BRep
indexed face-set publishing path, and rejects BSG geometry command tokens from
the evaluated-wireframe line-set builders.  It also rejects direct BSG overlay
geometry access from `src/libged/vdraw/vdraw.c` and prevents
`bsg_geometry_node_kind` from returning to the private overlay geometry record.
It also rejects direct BSG geometry updates and raw draw geometry-ref access
from `src/libged/draw.cpp` while requiring the BSG-backed draw source to own
the indexed-face update bridge.
Broader non-feature edit paths and lower-level BSG/geometry bridge cleanup
remain comparable cleanup candidates.

The libged `adc` command path now uses neutral `struct rt_view_adc_state`
records and RT-owned ADC coordinate helpers for model/view/grid conversion and
reset behavior.  The retained BSG faceplate ADC get/set calls are confined to
`src/librt/view_legacy_bsg.c`, which now exposes neutral
`rt_view_adc_state_from_bsg` and `rt_view_adc_state_set_bsg` adapters beside
the older BSG-shaped transitional adapters.  The pivot guard now requires the
neutral ADC record/helpers in `src/libged/adc/adc.c` and rejects
`bsg/adc.h`, `struct bsg_adc_state`, the old BSG-shaped ADC adapter calls, and
direct BSG ADC math helpers from returning to that command file.

The standalone libged `grid` command path now follows the same pattern for
faceplate grid state.  `src/libged/grid/grid.c` uses neutral
`struct rt_view_grid_state` records and `rt_view_grid_state_from_bsg` /
`rt_view_grid_state_set_bsg` while the BSG-shaped grid record and
`bsg_view_grid_get` / `bsg_view_grid_set` calls remain inside the RT legacy
view adapter.  The command's `vsnap` operation already used the RT snap/grid
adapter and was left unchanged.  The pivot guard now rejects the old
BSG-shaped grid record and adapter calls from returning to
`src/libged/grid/grid.c`.

The `view faceplate grid` command path now uses the same neutral grid record
and adapters for its top-level draw toggles and subcommand dispatch.  The

the pivot guard now rejects `struct bsg_grid_state`, the older BSG-shaped

`src/libged/view/faceplate/faceplate_grid.c`.

The libged faceplate center-dot, scale, and params command paths now use

records.  `src/libged/view/faceplate/faceplate.c` now calls neutral

The libged `view faceplate model_axes` and `view faceplate view_axes` command

The follow-up axes batch moved QGED view settings, qtcad Obol faceplate sync,

rejects the old axes wrapper calls from those migrated qged, qtcad, and

The libdm/MGED axes draw-boundary batch moved the public libdm axes drawing
helpers onto neutral `struct rt_view_axes_state` records.  `include/dm.h` no

The next faceplate settings batch moved the remaining QGED view settings

The TclCAD scalar faceplate batch moved `to_faceplate` center-dot,

The grid-state cleanup batch moved qged polygon grid-snapping controls,

The MGED ADC-state batch moved `mged_dm_adc_state_get/set`, the `adc`

BSG-shaped ADC RT wrappers for those paths.  The pivot guard now rejects the

lower-level libdm ADC draw signature was handled in the follow-up

The libdm ADC/grid draw-boundary cleanup moved the public `dm_draw_adc` and

The interactive rectangle draw-boundary cleanup moved the public

The RT legacy faceplate wrapper-surface cleanup removed the obsolete

The pivot guard now treats reintroducing the old BSG-shaped wrapper symbols
in the header, implementation, or focused test as a regression.

The next RT legacy wrapper-surface cleanup removed three more adapter/test-only
exports from `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`: `rt_view_selection_const_bsg`,

`rt_view_knob_state_hash_bsg`.  Production callers use the mutable selection
adapter, snap-exclude set/clear adapters, and view-level knob hash adapter

getter, from returning to the legacy header, implementation, or test.

The libged native-selection sync cleanup moved the remaining direct BSG
selection/interaction record handling out of `src/libged/selection_state.cpp`.
The BSG-backed GED draw bridge now owns helpers for selected-path enumeration,

selection-record escape hatch from returning to that file.

The qtcad selection-filter cleanup moved pick-result selection application and
view-selection clearing behind RT legacy selection helpers.  `QgSelectFilter`
still owns legacy BSG pick-result production for the staged pick migration,
but it no longer includes BSG selection/interaction headers, stores
interaction results, calls `rt_view_selection_bsg`, or calls

null, empty, callback-only, apply, and clear behavior for the new helpers, and
the pivot guard requires the helper route while rejecting direct selection API
use from the qtcad filter.

The qtcad selection-filter header cleanup made the protected selection helper
API view-implicit.  `QgSelectFilter::set_selected_result` and
`set_selected_paths` now resolve the installed filter view inside

application stays in the implementation behind the RT legacy adapter, and the

The qtcad selection pick-result fallback cleanup moved the remaining direct
BSG pick-result ownership in `QgSelectFilter.cpp` behind opaque RT legacy
pick-result adapters.  Point and rectangle fallback picks now call
`rt_view_pick_point_bsg`, `rt_view_pick_nearest_bsg`, and
`rt_view_pick_rect_bsg`; result count/path/hit-distance/free operations, first
result filtering, and ray-hit result copying use `rt_view_pick_result_*_bsg`
helpers; and selection application now uses
`rt_view_selection_set_pick_result_ref_bsg`.  The RT adapter still owns the
temporary BSG implementation, but qtcad no longer includes `bsg/pick.h`, names
`bsg_pick_result` or `bsg_pick_record`, or calls `bsg_pick_*` directly.  The
focused adapter test covers the opaque pick-result helpers and the pivot guard
now rejects direct BSG pick API use from the qtcad selection filter.

The RT legacy selection wrapper-surface cleanup retired the old public
`rt_view_selection_set_pick_result_bsg` export from `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`.
Selection pick application now exposes only
`rt_view_selection_set_pick_result_ref_bsg` for opaque

and interaction application stay private inside `src/librt/view_legacy_bsg.c`.
The focused adapter test constructs pick results through

the pivot guard now requires the opaque helper tokens while rejecting the
retired public setter from the RT adapter header, implementation, and test.

The libged draw-test pick cleanup added
`rt_view_pick_semantic_path_bsg` and moved the remaining direct BSG pick-result
use in `view_command.cpp` and `selection_semantics.cpp` behind the opaque RT
legacy pick-result helpers.  `view_command.cpp` now checks semantic-path pick
results via `rt_view_pick_result_count_bsg` and
`rt_view_pick_result_path_bsg`, while `selection_semantics.cpp` creates point
pick results with `rt_view_pick_point_bsg` and applies them through

`bsg/pick.h` and no longer name `bsg_pick_result`, `bsg_pick_record`, or call
`bsg_pick_*` directly.  The pivot guard now requires those RT adapter tokens
and rejects direct BSG pick/interaction access from the migrated test
surfaces.

The RT legacy selection getter cleanup retired the public
`rt_view_selection_bsg` selection-object export from `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`.
The RT adapter now exposes only `rt_view_selection_available_bsg`,
`rt_view_selection_count_bsg`, `rt_view_selection_clear_bsg`, and opaque
pick-result application for migrated selection callers; the raw
`bsg_selection` lookup is private to `src/librt/view_legacy_bsg.c`.  The

availability/count through those RT helpers and no longer include
