draw bridge still owns the staged BSG selection object for record-level
contains/add/enumeration paths, but its selection-count and clear helpers now
route through the RT count/clear helpers.  The pivot guard requires the new
selection availability/count tokens and rejects the retired RT getter from the
adapter header, implementation, focused test, qtcad selection filter, and
migrated libged selection test surfaces.

The GED-private selection bridge cleanup removed the remaining raw
`ged_draw_view_selection_bsg` getter from `bsg_ged_draw_view_private.h`.
`bsg_ged_draw_view.c` now owns the staged BSG selection object internally and
exposes narrower private helpers for selection availability, record contains,
record add, and record replacement.  `bsg_ged_draw_records.c` now uses those
record-level helpers for selected-state queries, highlighted-shape mirroring,
and selection adds; it no longer includes `bsg/selection.h`, names
`struct bsg_selection`, or calls `bsg_selection_*` directly.  The pivot guard
now rejects the retired private getter and direct BSG selection calls from the
records/header surfaces while still allowing the transitional implementation
inside the GED view bridge.

The public GED draw interaction-record cleanup removed
`ged_draw_shape_interaction_record` from `ged/bsg_ged_draw.h` and dropped that
header's `bsg/interaction.h` dependency.  The draw-scene selection bridge test
now verifies selected state through `ged_draw_shape_record_get` and the neutral
`selected` field instead of constructing a `bsg_interaction_record` and
querying `bsg_selection` directly.  `bsg_ged_draw_records.c` keeps the
temporary BSG interaction-record construction as a private helper for the
staged selection backing path.  The pivot guard now rejects the retired public
interaction-record helper and BSG interaction types/includes from the public
GED draw header and rejects direct BSG interaction/selection checks from the
migrated draw-scene test.

The qged/TclCAD low-risk cleanup removed two more direct BSG call-site leaks.
The revolve, extrude, and BOT edit-preview plugins now publish
begin/update/commit lifecycle events through `qged_edit_preview_util.h`; the
shared helper owns the temporary BSG interaction-record bridge, and the plugin
files no longer include `bsg/interaction.h` or call `bsg_interaction_*`
directly.  TclCAD polygon/mouse/command snap callers now store
`rt_view_prepare_tcl_snap_bsg` results in the adapter's neutral integer mask
type instead of naming `bsg_snap_kind_mask`.  The pivot guard checks both
boundaries.

The TclCAD polygon-mode naming cleanup restored TclCAD-owned circle and
contour mode constants in `include/tclcad/defines.h` and moved the remaining
polygon/command callers off `BSG_POLY_CIRCLE_MODE` and
`BSG_POLY_CONTOUR_MODE`.  The values are unchanged, and the pivot guard now
rejects those BSG mode names from returning to the TclCAD caller surface.

The qged/qtcad polygon view-level cleanup added narrow
`rt_view_polygon_create_bsg`, `rt_view_polygon_select_bsg`,
`rt_view_polygon_find_bsg`, `rt_view_polygon_dup_bsg`,
`rt_view_polygon_visit_records_bsg`, `rt_view_polygon_snap_count_bsg`, and
`rt_view_polygon_clear_point_selection_bsg` adapters.  The qged create/modify
tools and qtcad polygon create/select filters now route view-level polygon
creation, selection, lookup, duplication, enumeration, snap-count, and
point-selection-clear calls through the RT legacy view adapter.  The retained
BSG polygon view queries now live in the adapter with focused adapter-test
coverage, and the pivot guard rejects the direct calls from returning to the
qged/qtcad polygon caller surface.

The qtcad polygon type/update-name cleanup added qtcad-owned polygon type and
update-mode constants in `QgPolyFilter.h` and moved qged/qtcad polygon callers
off `BSG_POLYGON_GENERAL`, `BSG_POLYGON_CIRCLE`, `BSG_POLYGON_ELLIPSE`,
`BSG_POLYGON_RECTANGLE`, `BSG_POLYGON_SQUARE`, and
`BSG_POLYGON_UPDATE_*` names.  Numeric values are unchanged for the staged
BSG-backed polygon implementation.  The pivot guard now rejects those BSG
type/update names from the migrated qged/qtcad polygon caller surface while
leaving BSG polygon refs, records, and object mutation calls for the later
polygon-object migration.

The qtcad polygon ref-spelling cleanup narrowed `QgPolyFilter.h` away from the
broad `bsg/polygon.h` include for its public polygon-reference member and added
qtcad-owned `qg_polygon_ref` plus `QG_POLYGON_REF_NULL_INIT` aliases backed by
the existing feature-ref storage.  qged create/modify plugins and qtcad polygon
filters now use the qtcad-owned ref spelling in their public/private caller
surface.  The pivot guard rejects raw `bsg_polygon_ref` and
`BSG_POLYGON_REF_NULL_INIT` spelling from returning to that migrated polygon
surface while intentionally leaving BSG polygon predicates, records, and object
mutation calls staged for the later polygon-object migration.

The next qtcad/qged polygon-object cleanup added RT legacy adapters for the
remaining low-level BSG polygon object operations used by the Qt polygon tools:
reference null checks, record reads, update/update-from-screen, close/open,
view/style/name mutation, point-selection clear, move, CSG/remove, and
user-data get/set.  `QgPolyFilter`, `QPolyCreate`, and `QPolyMod` now call
those adapters rather than the BSG polygon object functions directly.  The
focused RT adapter test covers the new wrapper surface, and the pivot guard
rejects the direct BSG function calls from returning to the migrated polygon
caller surface.  The BSG polygon record struct and sketch import/export helpers
remain staged for a later neutral-record/sketch bridge cleanup.

The qtcad/qged polygon-record cleanup added the neutral
`rt_view_polygon_record` structure in `rt/view.h`, changed the RT legacy
polygon record getter and visitor callback to return that neutral record, and
confined BSG-to-RT record copying to `src/librt/view_legacy_bsg.c`.
`QgPolyFilter`, `QPolyCreate`, `QPolyMod`, and `QPolySettings` now use the
qtcad-owned `qg_polygon_record` alias and no longer include `bsg/polygon.h` or
name `struct bsg_polygon_record` directly.  The pivot guard now requires the
neutral/qtcad record tokens and rejects direct BSG polygon record spelling or
direct BSG polygon includes from the migrated qtcad/qged polygon caller
surface.  Sketch import/export remained the next staged polygon bridge cleanup.

The qged polygon sketch-bridge cleanup added
`rt_view_polygon_import_sketch_bsg` and `rt_view_polygon_export_sketch_bsg`
to the RT legacy view adapter.  `QPolyCreate` and `QPolyMod` now route sketch
import/export through those wrappers instead of including
`rt/primitives/sketch_legacy_bsg.h` or calling
`db_sketch_to_view_polygon_ref` / `db_view_polygon_ref_to_sketch` directly.
The lower-level BSG-backed sketch conversion implementation remains confined
to `src/librt/primitives/sketch/polygons_legacy_bsg.c`, and the pivot guard
rejects the direct sketch bridge from returning to qged polygon callers.

The qtcad/qged polygon-ref storage cleanup moved the caller-facing polygon ref
alias from `bsg_feature_ref` to RT-owned `rt_view_polygon_ref`.  The polygon
members, vectors, callbacks, and qged plugin state still use the local
`qg_polygon_ref` spelling, but that alias is now backed by `rt/view.h` rather
than `bsg/feature.h`.  The RT legacy polygon adapter converts between
`rt_view_polygon_ref` and BSG feature refs internally for the staged BSG-backed
implementation, and qged polygon line-snap exclusion now uses
`rt_view_polygon_snap_exclude_set_bsg` instead of passing polygon refs to the
generic feature-exclude setter.  The pivot guard rejects direct
`bsg_feature_ref` spelling and `bsg/feature.h` includes from the migrated
qtcad/qged polygon surface.

The qged polygon widget-boundary cleanup removed the private `bsg_view`
accessors and qtcad view/filter header includes from the create/modify/settings
widget headers.  `QPolyCreate`, `QPolyMod`, and `QPolySettings` now resolve the
active staged view through `QgPluginContext` inside implementation files, and
polygon view-name validation no longer reaches through `gedp->ged_gvp`.
`QPolySettings::uniq_obj_name` now also supports its validation-only null
output-buffer mode and releases its temporary name buffers, fixing the staged
UI collision check while preserving the RT legacy unique-name adapter boundary.

The qged view-plugin header/view-name cleanup added `rt_view_name_from_bsg`
to the RT legacy view adapter and covered normal, empty, and null view-name
queries in `rt_view_legacy_bsg`.  `CADViewModel` and `HostStatusCommon` now use
that adapter instead of reading `gv_name` directly.  `CADViewSelector` and
`CADViewMeasure` no longer include the qtcad filter headers or `ged.h` from
their widget headers, and their event filters resolve active staged views
through `QgPluginContext` instead of `gedp->ged_gvp`.  The pivot guard now
requires those routes and rejects direct field/header regressions from the
migrated qged view-plugin surface.

The qtcad plugin-context boundary cleanup removed the public `bsg_view`
forward declaration, `viewAccessor`, and `getView()` convenience wrapper from
`QgPluginContext`.  qged view/info/settings/status, polygon, and edit-preview
plugin implementations now resolve the remaining staged compatibility pointer
from `getViewWidget()` first and fall back to `QgModel::session()->activeView()`
for startup/null-host behavior.  The pivot guard now rejects public
`QgPluginContext` BSG active-view exposure and old qged plugin `getView()` call
sites.  After rebuilding the stale `test_qtcad_plugin_reuse` binary against the
new header layout, the plugin reuse test passed with the updated plugins.

The qtcad view-filter boundary cleanup removed the public raw
`QgViewFilter::set_view`/`view` API and the `struct bsg_view` forward
declaration from `QgViewFilter.h`.  `QgView::installFilter` now supplies only
the owning `QgView` widget, and `QgViewFilter::view_sync` plus the selection,
measurement, polygon, and sketch filter implementations derive the retained
compatibility view from `view_widget()` locally.  qged select/measure/polygon
plugins now set filter widgets rather than raw views, and the focused
pick/measure/snap tests do the same.  The pivot guard locks the public header
and install path against the retired raw filter view API.

The qtcad public-header spelling cleanup removed an unused `struct bsg_view`
forward declaration from `QgModel.h` and rewrote the stale `QgSketchFilter.h`
usage note to avoid exposing retained view pointers to callers.  The pivot
guard now rejects `bsg_view` spelling and BSG includes from both headers.

The qged framebuffer-server header cleanup removed the direct
`dm/fbserv.h` include from `src/qged/fbserv.h`.  A later tightening removed
the retained `fbserv_obj` type from that header entirely: the Qt helper
classes now keep opaque `void *` server state, while `src/qged/fbserv.cpp`
owns the retained libdm framebuffer-server include, callback signatures, and
field access.  The pivot guard now rejects libdm/BSG header exposure, retained
`fbserv_obj` spelling, and raw qdm framebuffer-server callback prototypes from
the qged framebuffer-server header while requiring the implementation file to
own the compatibility dependency.

The public GED fbserv include cleanup removed `dm/fbserv.h` from
`include/ged/defines.h`.  The retained `ged_fbs` member is still present for
the staged framebuffer-server bridge, but the public GED defines header now
forward declares `struct fbserv_obj`; `ged.cpp`, `ged_util.cpp`, qged app
setup, and ERT include the libdm fbserv header at the implementation boundary
where they allocate, dereference, or call the retained framebuffer-server API.
The pivot guard now rejects public `dm/fbserv.h` exposure from
`include/ged/defines.h`.

The qtcad input-binding cleanup deleted the obsolete `bindings.h` and
`bindings.cpp` free-function layer.  `QgCanvasInput` had already replaced that
API with per-canvas input handlers and per-instance drag-update state, and the
old files were no longer built or called.  `src/libqtcad/CMakeLists.txt` no
longer inventories the dead files, and the pivot guard now requires
`QgCanvasInput.cpp` as the only qtcad input adapter path while rejecting the
deleted files and `CAD*Event` free-function tokens from returning.

The follow-on qtcad input handle cleanup moved `QgCanvasInput.h` from retained
`struct bsg_view *` parameters to the opaque `qg_legacy_view *` boundary and
moved the drag bounds-update callback maps behind an implementation pimpl.
`QgCanvasInput.cpp` now owns the explicit `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` conversion and
the `rt_view_bounds_update_callback_bsg_t` storage needed by the retained
legacy view path.  `QgGL` and `QgSW` adapt their current staged BSG view only
at the input-handler call sites, and the pivot guard now rejects BSG view
spelling from `QgCanvasInput.h` while requiring the implementation-side
adapter tokens.

The qtcad/qged active-view sync cleanup added
`qg_obol_display_accepts_ged_active_view` as a narrow transitional helper for
Obol overlay, selection, and draw-observer display acceptance.  The helper
keeps the remaining GED active-view comparison in `QgObolViewSync.cpp`, while
`QgObolSelectionSync`, `QgObolOverlaySync`, and the qged observer handlers call
the helper instead of comparing `display->view()` with `gedp->ged_gvp`
directly.  The public helper header forward declares `struct ged` and `QgView`
without exposing BSG view types.  The same helper surface now includes
`qg_obol_display_accepts_draw_transaction_view`, keeping qged draw-observer
transaction-view scoping out of app code as well.  The pivot guard now enforces
both helper routes plus the CMake registrations.

The qtcad model/tree draw-state cleanup added `QgModel::drawnPathState` for
tree and model-display queries.  `QgTreeView::do_draw_toggle` now asks the
model for draw state instead of reading `m->ged()->ged_gvp` and calling
`ged_draw_path_state` itself, and `QgModel::data` routes `DrawnDisplayRole`
through the same helper.  `QgModel.cpp` keeps the remaining active-view lookup
behind a local `qgmodel_active_view` helper for draw-state queries and
draw/erase transaction setup.  The pivot guard now rejects direct GED/BSG
draw-state view access from `QgTreeView` and keeps the model's direct
`ged_gvp` usage confined to that helper.

The qgmodel model-tester draw-state cleanup moved the test's canonical draw
state assertions onto `QgModel::drawnPathState` as well.  The test no longer
includes `ged/bsg_ged_draw.h`, names `ged_draw_path_state`, or reads
`gedp->ged_gvp` / `event_gedp->ged_gvp`; direct GED command execution remains
where the event-transaction bridge itself is under test.  The pivot guard now
rejects those direct draw-state view-access patterns from returning to the
qtcad model tester.  Verified with `ninja -C build test_qgmodel_model_tester`
and CTest for `brlobol_pivot_guard` plus `test_qgmodel_model_tester`.

The qtcad session active-view cleanup added `QgSession::activeView` as the
session-owned transitional reader for GED's current BSG-backed view.  The
qtcad model draw-state helper now asks the session for the active view instead
of reading `ged_gvp`, and qged's plugin-context fallback view accessor uses
the same session method when the main window has not been constructed yet.
The write side is now owned by `QgSession::setActiveView` as well: qged's
startup current-view assignment and quad-view switch handling call the session
setter instead of writing `ged_gvp` directly.  The qged main-window public
interface no longer exposes `CurrentView()` as a BSG-returning method; qged app
code resolves the current display's staged view through an implementation-local
helper when it still needs the legacy view pointer.  The pivot guard now
requires the session read/write accessors, keeps `QgModel.cpp` and
`QgEdApp.cpp` free of direct `ged_gvp` reads/writes, and rejects the removed
main-window active-view surface.

The qtcad quad-view active-view cleanup moved `QgQuadView` from a raw GED
constructor argument to `QgSession`.  Quad-view still uses the GED context for
the staged BSG view set and for replaying visible paths into newly-created
quadrants, but startup active-view selection, temporary per-quadrant draw
propagation swaps, and quad-mode reset now use `QgSession::activeView` and
`QgSession::setActiveView` rather than writing `ged_gvp` directly.  The pivot
guard now rejects the raw-GED quad-view constructor and direct `ged_gvp` writes
from returning to the quad-view path.  The unused public
`QgQuadView::view(int)` accessor has also been removed, so `QgQuadView.h` no
longer exposes raw GED or BSG view spelling.

The qged app active-view cleanup continued by routing command-time unit
conversion and palette element dimension refresh through `QgSession::activeView`
instead of asking the current display widget for its staged `view()` pointer.
The pivot guard now rejects the old qged patterns that pulled `qged_current_view`
for unit conversion or called `rt_view_dimensions_set_bsg(curr_view->view(), ...)`.

The qged app legacy-view adapter cleanup added neutral qtcad helper functions
on the opaque `qg_legacy_view *` handle for dimension and unit-conversion
writes.  `QgLegacyView.cpp` now owns the explicit `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` cast and
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h` calls, while `QgEdApp.cpp` calls
`qg_legacy_view_dimensions_set` and `qg_legacy_view_unit_conversion_set` on the
session active view.  This removes the direct staged-BSG cast header and RT
legacy-view adapter include from qged app code for those command/palette paths.
The pivot guard now requires the new helper source/build registration and
rejects the old direct adapter tokens from `QgEdApp.cpp`.  Verified with
`ninja -C build libqtcad qged` and CTest for `brlobol_pivot_guard`,
`ged_test_qged_swrast`, and `test_qtcad_plugin_reuse`.

The qtcad view-control toolbar cleanup moved `QgViewCtrl` from a raw GED
pointer to `QgSession`.  Framebuffer clear/raytrace command paths still
retrieve the GED context from the session, while framebuffer mode toggle/icon
updates use `QgSession::activeView` through an implementation-local helper.
`QgViewCtrl.h` no longer exposes raw GED or BSG view spelling for that path,
and the pivot guard enforces the session constructor plus rejects direct
`ged_gvp` reads in the toolbar.

The qtcad view-control framebuffer-mode cleanup added neutral
`qg_legacy_view_framebuffer_mode_get` and
`qg_legacy_view_framebuffer_mode_set` helpers on the opaque legacy-view
handle.  `QgViewCtrl.cpp` now calls those helpers on the session active view
instead of including `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` and calling the RT legacy
framebuffer-mode adapters directly.  The pivot guard now requires the helper
surface and rejects the old direct adapter tokens from returning to the
toolbar.  Verified with `ninja -C build libqtcad qged` and CTest for
`brlobol_pivot_guard`, `ged_test_qged_swrast`, and
`test_qtcad_plugin_reuse`.

The qtcad GL/SW framebuffer-size cleanup extended `QgLegacyView.cpp` with
opaque width and height getters and routed `QgGL.cpp` / `QgSW.cpp` viewport
dimension synchronization through `qg_legacy_view_dimensions_set`,
`qg_legacy_view_width_get`, and `qg_legacy_view_height_get`.  The retained RT
dimension adapters remain in the qtcad helper implementation, while the
canvas widgets no longer call those get/set wrappers directly.  The pivot
guard now rejects `rt_view_dimensions_set_bsg`, `rt_view_width_from_bsg`, and
`rt_view_height_from_bsg` from returning to those widget files.  Verified with
`ninja -C build libqtcad qged` and CTest for `brlobol_pivot_guard`,
`ged_test_qged_swrast`, and `test_qtcad_plugin_reuse`.

The qtcad quad-view unit/size copy cleanup added opaque
`qg_legacy_view_local2base_get` and `qg_legacy_view_base2local_get` helpers
and moved `QgQuadView.cpp` initial quadrant unit/dimension copies plus the
post-draw-propagation dimension refresh onto the `QgLegacyView` helper
surface.  The remaining retained BSG view-set, LoD policy, and autoview calls
stay in `QgQuadView.cpp` for later slices, but direct RT unit/dimension
get/set wrappers are now confined to `QgLegacyView.cpp` for this path.  The
pivot guard rejects those wrappers from returning to the quad-view unit/size
copy path.  Verified with `ninja -C build libqtcad qged` and CTest for
`brlobol_pivot_guard`, `ged_test_drawing_quad`,
`ged_test_bsg_quad_stability`, `ged_test_qged_swrast`, and
`test_qtcad_plugin_reuse`.

The qged plugin-context active-view cleanup moved the startup/null-host
fallback from each plugin implementation into `QgPluginContext::activeView`.
The new accessor returns the existing opaque `qg_legacy_view *` handle: it
prefers the active `QgView` widget and falls back to `QgModel::session()` only
inside libqtcad.  View, polygon, host-status, and edit-preview qged plugins
now call `ctx->activeView()` and convert to the retained staged view pointer
only where they still invoke legacy RT/BSG APIs.  The pivot guard now requires
the opaque plugin-context accessor, keeps BSG conversion out of
`QgPluginContext.cpp`, and rejects qged plugins from reintroducing
open-coded `ctx->model->session()->activeView()` fallback logic.

The qtcad view-filter and draw-sync test include cleanup removed stale direct
BSG dependencies.  `QgViewFilter.cpp` now gets snap-source, snap-kind,
mouse-state, and tolerance access exclusively through `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`
instead of including `bsg/defines.h` directly.  The draw-sync test no longer
includes `bsg/util.h` and now routes active-view, transaction-view, center,
and scale setup through qtcad legacy-view helpers; it still uses
`ged/bsg_ged_draw.h` for the retained GED transaction records it intentionally
exercises.  The pivot guard now rejects stale direct BSG view setup from
returning to this path, and the focused build/tests passed:
`ninja -C build libqtcad qged`, `ninja -C build test_qtcad_obol_draw_sync`,
and CTest for `brlobol_pivot_guard`, `test_qtcad_obol_pick`,
`test_qtcad_obol_snap`, `test_qtcad_obol_measure`,
`test_qtcad_plugin_reuse`, and `test_qtcad_obol_draw_sync`.

The qtcad canvas/session legacy-view boundary cleanup introduced
`QgLegacyView.h` as the public opaque handle declaration and
`QgLegacyViewBsg.h` as the explicit transitional BSG adapter.  `QgCanvasBase`,
`QgView`, `QgSession`, `QgGL`, and `QgSW` now expose `qg_legacy_view *` rather
than `struct bsg_view *`; BSG conversion is confined to implementation files,
qged plugin helpers, the Qt libdm framebuffer bridges, and tests that
intentionally exercise retained staged BSG state.  The focused qtcad Obol
tests were updated to use the adapter explicitly, and the pivot guard now
requires the new headers while rejecting BSG view exposure from the main
public qtcad canvas/session headers.

The qtcad dense real-model test was split into named CTest entries with
explicit 180-second timeouts, and the dense m35 pick-all workflow is now a
separate `m35_wire_pick_all_stress` case.  The normal m35 interaction case
uses nearest-pick semantics for the identity assertions it actually needs,
while the stress case asserts multiple pick-all hits and nearest-first
ordering.  `QgObolPick.cpp` now appends pick-all records and stable-sorts
after Obol/source-detail/RT fallback records are merged, replacing the old
per-hit middle insertion path that made dense wireframe pick-all effectively
quadratic.  The pivot guard now requires the append/sort helper and rejects
the old insert loop, and the focused qtcad suite passed with the stress case
under the per-test cap.

The qtcad private canvas-state boundary now stores opaque `qg_legacy_view *`
handles for both the active view and the widget-owned local view.  `QgGL` and
`QgSW` allocate/free the retained BSG view through explicit local adapter
variables, return the opaque handle from `view()`, and pass that opaque handle
to `qgcanvas_set_view`; legacy RT/DM calls convert at the implementation
boundary through `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` and the `qgcanvas_bsg_view` helpers.
The pivot guard now requires `QgCanvasState.h` to keep the opaque fields and
rejects reverting `qgcanvas_set_view` to a raw `struct bsg_view *` signature.
The shared canvas-state dimension sync and canvas input motion-scale height
read now use the opaque qtcad legacy-view helpers too, so those direct RT
legacy BSG size wrappers are no longer named in the canvas state/input files.
The qtcad view, sketch, and selection filters now use the opaque legacy-view
width/height helpers for snap tolerance, vertex-snap scaling, and
box-selection rectangle dimensions.  Their remaining BSG adapter use is
limited to the retained staged snap, transform, selection, and pick APIs.
The QGED view settings plugin now uses the same opaque qtcad legacy-view
helper surface for LoD policy, framebuffer mode, and ADC/grid/axes/scalar
faceplate state get/set operations.  `CADViewSettings.cpp` still edits
neutral `rt/view.h` records, but it no longer includes the qtcad BSG adapter,
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, or direct RT legacy-BSG state wrappers.
The qged view-info and host-status plugins now use the opaque qtcad
legacy-view helper surface for view info, view name, AET, and center reads.
Those small qged plugins no longer include `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`; the retained read-only RT legacy-BSG calls are
confined to `QgLegacyView.cpp`.
The qged measure and selector plugins now use opaque `qg_legacy_view *`
handles for their active-view presence checks instead of converting to
`struct bsg_view *`.  The actual measurement and selection interaction logic
remains in the libqtcad filters, but the qged plugin files no longer include
`QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, call `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, or spell `bsg_view`.
The qged polygon settings widget now routes view-object unique-name checks
through `qg_legacy_view_unique_object_name`, leaving the retained
`rt_view_unique_object_name_bsg` call in `QgLegacyView.cpp`.  `QPolySettings`
no longer includes the qtcad BSG adapter or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`.
The qged polygon create/modify plugins now route their remaining view-backed
polygon and snap operations through `QgLegacyView.h` as well.  `QgLegacyView`
owns wrappers for polygon duplicate/find/visit, sketch import/export, snap
source/line/exclude state, grid snap state, alignment view writes, polygon
ref metadata, CSG, visual updates, removal, and user data.  `QPolyCreate.cpp`
and `QPolyMod.cpp` no longer include `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, call `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spell
`struct bsg_view`, or call the RT legacy-BSG polygon/view wrappers directly.
The pivot guard now requires those qtcad helper routes and rejects direct BSG
adapter tokens from returning to the qged polygon create/modify files.
The same wrapper surface now covers `QgPolyFilter.cpp`: screen-point
conversion, polygon create/select, screen-point updates, close, move,
name/visual updates, CSG, removal, and record/ref checks route through
`QgLegacyView.h`.  The filter no longer includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells
`struct bsg_view`, or names retained RT legacy-BSG polygon helpers directly.
FreeBSD/clang caught that the screen-point adapter needs a mutable staged
view handle, so `qg_legacy_view_screen_point_get` intentionally takes
`qg_legacy_view *`.
The qtcad measurement filter now uses that same opaque helper boundary for
its remaining staged view reads.  `QgLegacyView.h` owns screen-to-view and
view-to-model wrappers, and `QgMeasureFilter.cpp` no longer includes
`QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells `struct bsg_view`, or names
`rt_view_screen_to_view_from_bsg` / `rt_view_view2model_from_bsg` directly.
The pivot guard requires those qtcad helper calls and rejects the direct
measurement adapter tokens from returning.
The qtcad sketch filter now follows the same pattern for its remaining
retained view reads.  `QgLegacyView.h` owns the added model-to-view wrapper
beside the screen-to-view, screen-point, and width helpers, and
`QgSketchFilter.cpp` no longer includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells
`struct bsg_view`, or names `rt_view_model2view_from_bsg`,
`rt_view_screen_to_view_from_bsg`, or `rt_view_screen_point_from_bsg`
directly.  The pivot guard now requires the opaque sketch helper tokens and
rejects the old direct adapter path.
Building the standalone `qsketch` fixture on FreeBSD/clang exposed a retained
test/demo boundary mismatch: its retained staged view setup had not yet
matched `QgView::set_view`'s opaque `qg_legacy_view *` API.  The first fix
adapted the raw view through the explicit `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` boundary; the
later qsketch cleanup moved local view ownership and routine view-state setup
onto the qtcad legacy-view helper surface, leaving that private bridge only
for retained edit and sketch line-set scene graph calls.
The shared qtcad view filter is now on the opaque helper boundary as well.
`QgLegacyView.h` owns qtcad-local snap kind constants plus helpers for DB snap
eligibility, snap kind mask translation, view size, snap tolerance factor,
current point get/set, and mouse-state synchronization.  `QgViewFilter.cpp`
uses those helpers for Obol snap refinement and event-stream synchronization,
so it no longer includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells `struct bsg_view`, names RT legacy-BSG snap
policy helpers, or names `RT_VIEW_SNAP_*_BSG` constants directly.  The pivot
guard now requires the qtcad helper route and rejects the direct shared-filter
adapter path.

The qtcad session and Obol active-view sync boundary now use the same opaque
legacy-view helper surface.  `QgLegacyView.h` exposes helpers to get/set GED's
active staged view and to read a draw transaction's scoped view as
`qg_legacy_view *`, leaving the retained BSG cast and direct `ged_gvp` /
`txn->view` reads in `QgLegacyView.cpp`.  `QgSession.cpp` and
`QgObolViewSync.cpp` no longer include `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, call
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg` / `qg_legacy_view_from_bsg`, or read those raw GED draw
view fields directly.  The pivot guard now requires the opaque helper route
and rejects direct BSG/GED active-view access from returning to those files.
`QgView.cpp` now follows that same boundary for semantic refresh requests:
qtcad-local `QG_LEGACY_VIEW_REFRESH_*` flag names replace direct
`RT_VIEW_REFRESH_*_BSG` spelling, and the staged-view refresh request goes
through `qg_legacy_view_refresh_request`.  The wrapper widget no longer
includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, and the pivot guard
locks that helper route while leaving the lower canvas backend internals on
their retained RT legacy refresh path.
The qtcad input adapter also moved its simple key-driven view state updates
onto the opaque helper surface: `QgCanvasInput::keyPressEvent` now toggles
ADC, model axes, and view axes through `qg_legacy_view_*_state_get/set`
helpers and applies AET view presets through `qg_legacy_view_aet_set` plus
`qg_legacy_view_update`.  The mouse/wheel adjustment path, drag-center lookup,
and drag bounds callback suspend/restore now go through
`qg_legacy_view_adjust`, `qg_legacy_view_center_get`, and an opaque
`qg_legacy_view_bounds_update_state` token, so `QgCanvasInput.cpp` no longer
includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells `struct bsg_view`, or names the retained RT
legacy input adapter functions.  The pivot guard requires the input helper
route and rejects the old direct RT legacy faceplate/AET/adjust/bounds calls
from returning.
`QgModel.cpp` now uses the same opaque helper surface for draw-view scoping:
drawn-state queries call `qg_legacy_view_draw_path_state`, blank-slate draw
checks call `qg_legacy_view_draw_has_paths`, and draw/erase transactions set
their scoped view through `qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_view_set`.  The
retained `ged_draw_path_state`, `ged_draw_has_paths`, and transaction
`bsg_view *` assignment stay inside `QgLegacyView.cpp`; the model no longer
includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, calls `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, or spells
`struct bsg_view`.  The pivot guard rejects those direct draw-view access
patterns from returning to `QgModel.cpp`.

The Obol draw transaction sync now consumes that helper surface too.
`QgLegacyView.h` exposes `qg_legacy_view_draw_list_paths`, and
`QgObolDrawSync.cpp` gets transaction-scoped views through
`qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_view_get`, enumerates full-scene paths
through `qg_legacy_view_draw_list_paths`, and resolves per-path draw mode with
`qg_legacy_view_draw_path_state`.  The sync implementation no longer includes
`QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, calls `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells `struct bsg_view`,
or calls `ged_draw_path_state` / `ged_draw_list_paths` directly.  The pivot
guard locks that route while leaving the retained GED transaction mode
constants in place.

qtcad mouse-adjust mode spelling has also moved behind the opaque legacy-view
boundary.  `QgLegacyView.h` now exposes `QG_LEGACY_VIEW_ADJUST_*` values that
are numeric-compatible with the retained RT adjustment backend, and the qtcad
input/controller/canvas initialization code uses those names instead of
`RT_VIEW_ADJUST_*`.  `QgViewCtrl.cpp` no longer includes
`rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`; the remaining canvas backend files keep that include
only for retained BSG view construction and DM/libdm bridge operations.  The
pivot guard now requires the qtcad adjust constants and rejects direct RT
adjust constants from the migrated qtcad paths.

The qtcad quad-view lifecycle now follows the same opaque helper pattern.
`QgLegacyView.h` exposes helpers for staged view naming, GED view-set
add/remove/attach, LoD policy copy, default autoview, and LoD bounds update.
`QgQuadView.cpp` uses those helpers for quadrant setup and layout transitions,
so it no longer includes `QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells `struct bsg_view`, or names the retained RT
view-set/LoD/autoview adapters directly.  The pivot guard now requires that
helper route and removes `QgQuadView.cpp` from the direct transitional
view-set allowlist.

The long `havoc_wire` qtcad real-model runtime was traced with
`BRLOBOL_QTCAD_REAL_MODEL_TIMING=1`.  Before the fix the case spent about
22.2 seconds in the legacy GED draw transaction, about 0.5 seconds in Obol
sync, about 0.1 seconds in render/readback, and about 49.0 seconds in the
test helper's geometry-count validation.  That helper was quadratic because
it called `getRealizedShape(i)` for each realized shape, and each indexed
lookup recursively searched the scene graph from the root.  The test now
counts realized wire shapes/segments and mesh shapes/triangles with one
recursive traversal, preserving the validation while reducing the count phase
to about 0.010 seconds.  The focused qtcad suite now reports `havoc_wire` at
about 23.6 seconds and 20/20 focused tests pass in about 50 seconds.

The qged edit-preview feature-boundary cleanup moved the ELL, revolve,
extrude, and BOT plugin feature refs and overlay operations behind
`qged_edit_preview_util.h`.  The plugins now use `qged_edit_feature_ref` plus
helper calls for preview overlay ensure/remove, visibility, view assignment,
color/touch updates, lifecycle callback attachment, and ELL edit labels.  The
plugin files no longer include `bsg/feature.h`, `bsg/overlay.h`, or
`bsg/hud.h`, name `bsg_feature_ref`, or call `bsg_feature_*` directly.  The
helper now uses qged-owned line-command tokens for move/draw/point preview
geometry, so it no longer includes `bsg/geometry.h` or spells
`BSG_GEOMETRY_*` command names.  The retained BSG feature/overlay operations
have also been moved out of that header into
`qged_edit_preview_util.cpp`, so `qged_edit_preview_util.h` no longer includes
`bsg/feature.h`, names `bsg_feature_ref` / `bsg_edit_preview_ops`, or spells
`BSG_FEATURE_*`, `BSG_OVERLAY_*`, or `BSG_ANCHOR_*` constants directly.  The
edit-preview interaction-record create/free bridge has also been moved out of
that header into `qged_edit_preview_util.cpp`, so `qged_edit_preview_util.h`
no longer includes `bsg/interaction.h`, names `struct bsg_interaction_record`,
or maps `BSG_EDIT_PREVIEW_*` operation constants directly.  The pivot guard
now enforces that boundary for the four edit-preview plugins while continuing
to require update/commit lifecycle events to publish through the helper.  The
same qged edit-preview cleanup removed the stale direct `ged/bsg_ged_draw.h`
includes from the ELL, revolve, extrude, and BOT plugin implementations and
removed their private `bsg_view`-returning `getView()` declarations from the
plugin headers.  The shared qged edit-preview helper API now takes
`QgPluginContext` for staged view access, adapts qged-owned callback signatures
to BSG callbacks inside `qged_edit_preview_util.cpp`, and keeps direct
`bsg_view` spelling out of the helper header and plugin files.

The qtcad selection filter now routes its remaining staged pick and view
snapshot work through `QgLegacyView.h`.  `QgLegacyView` owns opaque
`qg_legacy_view_pick_result` handles plus helpers for point/nearest/rectangle
fallback picks, result create/free/count/path/hit-distance/copy/filter-first,
selection apply/clear, interactive rectangle get/set, and screen-ray
construction.  `QgSelectFilter.h` no longer forward declares
`rt_view_pick_result_bsg`, and `QgSelectFilter.cpp` no longer includes
`QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`, calls
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spells `struct bsg_view`, or calls direct
`rt_view_*_bsg` pick/selection/view snapshot helpers.  The retained RT legacy
BSG calls now live in `QgLegacyView.cpp`, and the pivot guard has been flipped
to require the qtcad helper route while rejecting direct BSG adapter tokens
from returning to the selection filter.

The qged application draw-observer boundary now follows the same opaque helper
route.  `QgLegacyView` exposes qtcad-local helpers for GED draw observer
registration/removal, manual redraw transactions, and transaction view-presence
checks; `QgEdApp.cpp` uses those helpers instead of including
`ged/bsg_ged_draw.h`, calling `ged_draw_observer_*`,
`ged_draw_transaction_make`, or `ged_draw_apply_transaction` directly, or
reading `txn->view`.  The retained GED draw adapter calls and transaction field
access are confined to `QgLegacyView.cpp`, and the pivot guard now requires
the helper route while rejecting those direct tokens from returning to qged.

The qtcad canvas-state/backend bridge moved another batch of staged view access
behind `QgLegacyView`.  `QgLegacyView` now owns local view create/free helpers,
refresh consume/complete, rotation/scale/perspective/hash reads, display-manager
attachment, and scale-storage access.  `QgCanvasState.h` uses those helpers for
Obol camera and faceplate snapshots, view-hash diffing, refresh requests, AET
updates, and external-view display-manager attachment; `QgGL.cpp` and
`QgSW.cpp` use them for widget-local view lifetime, refresh completion, DM
scale coupling, and SW screenshot model2view loads.  Those three files no
longer include `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h` or call the direct RT legacy-view wrappers;
the remaining `qgcanvas_bsg_view` bridge is confined to lower libdm entry points
that still require the retained BSG view pointer.  The local-free helper keeps
the historical qtcad storage-release behavior, because these canvas views can
outlive GED scene cleanup during shutdown/test teardown and full retained-view
cleanup can traverse stale scene-node refs in that ordering.

QgModel's draw transaction boundary has also moved behind `QgLegacyView`.
`QgLegacyView` now exposes helper accessors for draw scene revisions, draw
result names, transaction path lists, and material/view-only transaction
classification, plus qtcad-local draw/erase apply helpers that own GED draw
transaction result lifetime internally.  `QgModel.cpp` uses those helpers for
observer registration/removal, command draw-revision checks, deferred draw
notification coalescing, and model-driven draw/erase actions.  It no longer
includes `ged/bsg_ged_draw.h`, calls `ged_draw_observer_*`,
`ged_draw_scene_revision`, `ged_draw_transaction_make`,
`ged_draw_apply_transaction`, or GED draw result init/free directly, names
`GED_DRAW_TXN_*`, or reads `txn->...` / `result->names` fields.

The Obol draw-sync transaction boundary now follows the same helper route.
`QgLegacyView` exposes qtcad-local draw mode constants, qtcad-local draw
transaction kind names, transaction kind/path/mode helpers, and draw-result
status/revision helpers, plus a GED database accessor for sync paths that need
`db_i` without reading the public GED struct directly.  `QgObolDrawSync.cpp`
uses those helpers for result success filtering, revision folding, transaction
path extraction, default draw mode resolution, database-source lookup, and sync
policy dispatch.  It no longer includes
`ged/bsg_ged_draw.h`, names `GED_DRAW_TXN_*` or `BSG_DRAW_MODE_*`, calls
`ged_draw_default_mode` directly, reads `gedp->dbip`, or reads `txn->...` /
`result->...` fields.
The pivot guard now requires the helper route and rejects those direct tokens
from returning to the Obol draw-sync implementation.

The ELL edit plugin draw-highlight boundary now also routes through
`QgLegacyView`.  `QgLegacyView` exposes helpers for highlighting a drawn shape
by name and clearing the current highlighted draw shape; `QEll.cpp` uses those
helpers instead of calling `ged_draw_highlight_shape_ref_by_name`,
`ged_draw_set_highlighted_shape_ref`, or naming `GED_DRAW_SHAPE_REF_NULL`
directly.  The pivot guard now requires that helper route for the plugin and
rejects the direct GED draw-highlight tokens from returning.

The qtcad fallback canvas DM bridge has been pulled out of the canvas files.
`QgLegacyView` now exposes `qg_legacy_view_dm_open_context` and
`qg_legacy_view_dm_draw`, which own the retained BSG cast and the
`dm_draw_objs` handoff needed by the temporary libdm fallback.  `QgCanvasState.h`
now includes only `QgLegacyView.h`, and `QgGL.cpp` / `QgSW.cpp` no longer
include `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, call `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, spell
`struct bsg_view`, call `dm_draw_objs` directly, or use the old
`qgcanvas_bsg_view` helper.  The pivot guard now requires the named
`QgLegacyView` DM bridge helpers and rejects the direct canvas-side BSG adapter
tokens from returning.

The qtcad canvas DM state boundary now also routes retained libdm hash,
native-repaint, configure-window, and size get/set operations through
`QgLegacyView` helpers.  This is a structural cleanup more than a behavior
change: `QgCanvasState.h` no longer includes `dm.h` or defines `DM_WITH_RT`,
so shared canvas state can carry only opaque DM/FB/PTBL handles while hash
diffing, repaint wakeups, external-view attachment, and size synchronization
use qtcad-local helper names.  `QgGL.cpp` and `QgSW.cpp` still include `dm.h`
for the retained fallback backend lifecycle, draw begin/end, framebuffer
readback, and backend-specific setup they still own, but their direct
hash/native-repaint/configure-window/size calls now go through the same bridge.
The pivot guard now requires those helpers and rejects the migrated direct DM
state calls from returning to the qtcad canvas layer.

The retained canvas DM/view binding sequence has also been lifted into the same
bridge.  `QgLegacyView` now exposes `qg_legacy_view_dm_bind`, which owns the
temporary `dm_set_vp` scale pointer handoff and the retained BSG view
display-manager assignment, plus `qg_legacy_view_dm_sync_dimensions`, which
copies current DM dimensions back into the legacy view record.  `QgGL.cpp`,
`QgSW.cpp`, and `QgCanvasState.h` use those semantic helpers instead of
assembling scale storage, display-manager assignment, and width/height sync
inline.  This keeps the remaining fallback backend files responsible for
opening/configuring/drawing the retained DM, while the fragile view/DM coupling
stays in the qtcad legacy-view bridge that can later be replaced by
Obol/libbrlobol state.  The pivot guard now rejects `dm_set_vp`,
`qg_legacy_view_scale_storage_get`, and direct display-manager setter use from
returning to the canvas layer.

The retained canvas existing-framebuffer setup details have also moved behind
the same bridge.  `QgLegacyView` owns
`qg_legacy_view_dm_framebuffer_setup_existing`, which owns the
`fb_platform_specific` record creation, DM-dimension lookup, and
`fb_setup_existing` call used by the libdm fallback.  The temporary public
display-manager-set hook and its `bu_ptbl_ins_unique` insertion path have since
been retired entirely from the qtcad canvas surface.  `QgGL.cpp` and
`QgSW.cpp` no longer include `bu/ptbl.h`, call `bu_ptbl_ins_unique`, or build
the existing-framebuffer setup record inline.  The pivot guard now requires the
helper route and rejects those lower-level retained setup calls from returning
to the canvas implementations.

Raw qtcad fallback framebuffer lifetime handling has also moved into
`QgLegacyView`.  The bridge now exposes helpers for raw framebuffer creation
with the historical non-standalone marking, retained framebuffer release
(`fb_close_existing` after initialization, `fb_put` before initialization),
standalone checks used by the right-click close behavior, and framebuffer resize
configuration.  `QgGL.cpp` and `QgSW.cpp` no longer call `fb_raw`,
`fb_set_standalone`, `fb_get_standalone`, `fb_close_existing`, `fb_put`, or
`fb_configure_window` directly.  This keeps the qtcad widgets on opaque
`struct fb *` ownership while the retained libdm framebuffer lifecycle remains
centralized in the bridge.  The pivot guard now rejects those framebuffer
lifecycle calls from returning to the canvas implementations.

The retained qtcad fallback DM open/close lifecycle is now part of the bridge
as well.  `QgLegacyView` owns typed open helpers for qtgl and swrast, including
the swrast legacy-view open context and the app-widget `dm_set_udata` handoff
used by framebuffer redraw events, plus the retained `dm_close` call.  `QgGL.cpp`
and `QgSW.cpp` now call `qg_legacy_view_dm_open_qtgl`,
`qg_legacy_view_dm_open_swrast`, and `qg_legacy_view_dm_close` instead of
calling `dm_open`, `dm_set_udata`, or `dm_close` directly.  The pivot guard now
requires the typed helper route and rejects those lifecycle calls from returning
to the canvas implementations.

Retained qtcad fallback backend setup policy is now typed through the bridge as
well.  `QgLegacyView` exposes `qg_legacy_view_dm_setup_qtgl` and
`qg_legacy_view_dm_setup_swrast`; these helpers own the qtgl/SW display-manager
path names, the SW zbuffer enable, and the temporary libdm window-bound setup
using the canvas-provided near/far values.  `QgGL.cpp` and `QgSW.cpp` now call
the typed setup helpers instead of spelling `dm_set_pathname`,
`dm_set_zbuffer`, or `dm_set_win_bounds` directly.  The pivot guard requires
the backend-specific setup helper route and rejects those retained setup calls
from returning to the canvas implementations.

Draw-time retained background and draw-boundary calls now route through
`QgLegacyView` as well.  The bridge exposes copied background get/set helpers,
a background-restore helper for the normal repaint clear path, and draw
begin/end helpers.  `QgGL.cpp` and `QgSW.cpp` no longer call `dm_get_bg`,
`dm_set_bg`, `dm_draw_begin`, or `dm_draw_end` directly; the SW screenshot path
still applies its dark-background policy, but it does so through copied
qtcad-local background arrays instead of raw libdm background pointers.  The
pivot guard now requires the helper route and rejects those draw-time libdm
calls from returning to the canvas implementations.

The qged edit-preview helper now routes retained BSG feature and interaction
operations through semantic `QgLegacyView` helpers instead of owning the bridge
itself.  `QgLegacyView` defines neutral feature refs, feature families,
edit-preview lifecycle events, callback records, and label records, and owns the
conversion to `bsg_feature_ref`, `bsg_edit_preview_ops`, BSG overlay owner
registration, edit-preview interaction record creation, label replacement, point
replacement, and clear/remove/style calls.  `qged_edit_preview_util.cpp` no
longer includes `bsg/feature.h` or `bsg/interaction.h`, spells raw `bsg_view`,
maps `BSG_EDIT_PREVIEW_*` / `BSG_FEATURE_*` / `BSG_OVERLAY_*` /
`BSG_ANCHOR_*`, or calls `bsg_feature_*` / `bsg_interaction_*` directly.  The
temporary raw `qg_legacy_view_edit_preview_context` escape hatch has been
removed, and the pivot guard now requires the `QgLegacyView` helper route while
rejecting direct BSG edit-preview bridge access from returning to qged.

The qtcad draw-observer transaction type boundary has also stopped publishing
raw GED draw transaction names outside the legacy adapter.  `QgLegacyView.h`
now defines qtcad-local aliases for the retained GED draw transaction/result
types, and `QgModel`, `QgObolDrawSync`, `QgObolViewSync`, and the qged Obol
draw observer use those aliases in their signatures.  This does not change the
underlying retained GED observer ABI yet; it narrows the visible type boundary
so future Obol/libbrlobol transaction carriers can replace the backend without
rewriting qtcad-facing declarations.  The pivot guard now requires the
qtcad-local transaction names in those surfaces and rejects direct
`struct ged_draw_transaction` / `struct ged_draw_transaction_result` exposure
from returning outside `QgLegacyView` and tests.

The qged framebuffer-server setup boundary has also been tightened.  The qged
app previously included `dm/fbserv.h` and `dm.h`, wrote the retained
`gedp->ged_fbs` callback slots directly, and read `dm_init_msgs()` itself.
`fbserv.cpp` now owns that libdm/fbserv bridge: it exposes
`qdm_configure_ged_fbserv_handlers` to install the correct TCP/IPC client
callbacks for the current qtcad view type, and `qdm_init_messages` to surface
the filtered DM startup diagnostics without making `QgEdApp.cpp` include libdm
headers.  Behavior is unchanged, but the qged application object no longer
owns retained framebuffer-server table details.  The pivot guard now requires
the bridge route and rejects direct `ged_fbs` or `dm_init_msgs` use from
returning to `QgEdApp.cpp`.

The qtcad canvas fallback display-manager boundary is now complete enough that
`QgGL.cpp` and `QgSW.cpp` no longer include `dm.h` or define `DM_WITH_RT`.
Their remaining retained fallback work is expressed through `QgLegacyView`:
typed qtgl/swrast open/setup/close helpers, framebuffer setup and release
helpers, size/hash/repaint helpers, background and draw-boundary helpers, SW
display-image readback and release helpers, and a semantic helper to load the
current legacy model2view matrix into the SW display manager.  The raw
`dm_get_display_image`, `dm_loadmatrix`, and image-buffer `bu_free` details now
live beside the other retained libdm calls in `QgLegacyView.cpp`.  The pivot
guard now requires the helper route in the canvas implementations and rejects
direct `dm.h`, `DM_WITH_RT`, display-image, matrix-load, and lower-level
framebuffer/display-manager lifecycle calls from returning there.

Those retained qtcad fallback declarations are now private to libqtcad as
well.  The installed `include/qtcad/QgLegacyView.h` no longer forward-declares
`struct dm`, `struct fb`, or `struct bu_ptbl`, and no longer declares the
retained DM/fb helpers, scale-storage pointer accessor, or direct
display-manager setter.  The helper declarations moved to
`src/libqtcad/QgLegacyViewDm.h`, which is included only by the libqtcad
implementation path.  The underlying retained calls still live in
`QgLegacyView.cpp`; this slice narrows the public qtcad surface so downstream
code cannot accidentally depend on the temporary DM/fb bridge while the Obol
replacement matures.  The pivot guard now enforces both halves of that boundary:
the public header must remain free of the retained DM/fb helper names, and the
private header must carry the transitional declarations without `QTCAD_EXPORT`.

The higher-level qtcad canvas/view surface has also stopped publishing retained
DM/fb handles.  `QgCanvasBase` now exposes `legacyBackendInitialized()` as the
neutral readiness query used by `QgView`; it no longer has virtual
`displayManager()` or `frameBuffer()` accessors.  `QgView` now forwards that
neutral readiness state, no longer declares `dmp()` / `ifp()`, and no longer
accepts a retained framebuffer pointer in its public constructor.  The qtcad
Obol tests that only needed to assert "the legacy fallback was not initialized"
now call `legacyBackendInitialized()` instead of checking a raw DM pointer.
The concrete `QgGL`/`QgSW` raw getters remain temporarily for backend-specific
tests that still inspect retained DM readback directly; those are now the next
smaller public-surface cleanup target rather than being reachable through the
generic `QgView` abstraction.  The pivot guard rejects raw DM/fb accessors from
returning to the `QgCanvasBase`/`QgView` boundary.

That concrete canvas cleanup is now done as well.  The installed `QgGL.h` and
`QgSW.h` headers no longer forward-declare `struct dm` and no longer declare
`displayManager()` / `frameBuffer()` getters.  They retain the neutral
`legacyBackendInitialized()` query.  The qtcad controller and swrast integration
tests that only needed to assert fallback initialization state now use the
neutral query or `get_viewport_image()` instead of raw DM readback.  The
currently inactive DM backend benchmark remains intentionally retained-DM
oriented, but it now obtains the retained display manager and performs draw
timing through the qtcad private DM bridge rather than public canvas API,
direct BSG casts, or direct libdm calls.  The pivot guard now rejects raw DM
getter declarations from returning to the concrete canvas public headers.

The framebuffer-backed concrete canvas constructor exposure has now been
removed from the installed qtcad headers as well.  `QgGL` and `QgSW` publish
only the parent-widget constructor; the retained qtgl/swrast framebuffer
windows construct framebuffer-backed canvases through the private
`QgCanvasBridgeFactory` declarations in `src/libqtcad/QgLegacyViewDm.h`.
The libdm qtgl/swrast plugin directories
include that private qtcad bridge header for the transitional construction
hook, while `include/qtcad/QgGL.h` and `include/qtcad/QgSW.h` no longer
forward-declare `struct fb` or publish retained framebuffer constructor
parameters.  The pivot guard now checks both sides: the installed concrete
canvas headers must stay free of raw DM/fb exposure, and the libdm framebuffer
window wrappers must keep using the private bridge factory instead of direct
`new QgGL(this, fbp)` / `new QgSW(this, fbp)` construction.

The standalone qtgl/swrast Qt window headers have also been narrowed to the
same boundary.  `qtglwin.h` and `swrastwin.h` no longer include `dm.h`,
concrete qtcad canvas headers, or `struct fb`; they take the opaque qtcad
`qg_legacy_fb` handle and forward-declare only the canvas class.  The
corresponding implementation files continue to include the private
`QgLegacyViewDm.h` bridge plus the concrete canvas headers and construct
canvases through `QgCanvasBridgeFactory`.  The pivot guard now rejects `dm.h`,
`dm/` includes, `struct dm`, `struct fb`, or concrete qtcad canvas includes
from returning to those window headers.

The private qtcad retained-DM bridge now keeps its own declarations opaque as
well.  `QgLegacyViewDm.h` declares distinct opaque `struct qg_legacy_dm` and
`struct qg_legacy_fb` handle types instead of publishing `struct dm *` and
`struct fb *` through the private qtcad canvas state API.  `QgCanvasState`,
`QgGL`, and `QgSW` store and pass those opaque handles, while
`QgLegacyView.cpp` remains the single place that casts them back to retained
libdm/libfb types and calls the legacy functions.  The standalone framebuffer
wrappers now convert raw framebuffer pointers through
`qg_legacy_view_framebuffer_handle_from_raw` and use the qtcad
`qg_legacy_view_dm_open_*` helpers for both swrast and qtgl, with qtgl's
direct `dm_open`/`dm_close` calls removed from `fb-qtgl.cpp`.  The pivot guard
now rejects typed or weak-typedef DM/fb exposure from the bridge
header/canvas state and rejects direct `dm_open`/`dm_close` or local
`qg_legacy_fb` reinterpret casts from the qtgl/swrast framebuffer wrappers.

The swrast integration test expectation was adjusted to match the migration
state exposed by that cleanup.  `QgSW::paintEvent()` is allowed to satisfy
migrated content through Obol readback without opening the retained fallback
DM; the test now requires either migrated Obol image output or retained
fallback initialization, and verifies non-background pixels when a rendered
image is available.  This preserves coverage of visible qged SW output without
forcing the legacy backend to initialize for content that no longer needs it.

The last public shared-display-manager-table hook on the qtcad canvas
interface has also been removed.  `QgCanvasBase`, `QgGL`, and `QgSW` no longer
declare `setDisplayManagerSet(struct bu_ptbl *)`; `QgCanvasState` no longer
stores a caller-owned DM table pointer; and the private
`qg_legacy_view_dm_set_add` helper plus the stale `bu/ptbl.h` include in
`QgLegacyView.cpp` have been retired.  No in-tree qtcad/qged caller was using
that API after the earlier quad-view/session cleanup, so this narrows the
installed canvas surface without changing runtime behavior.  The pivot guard
now rejects `setDisplayManagerSet` and public `struct bu_ptbl` exposure from
returning to the canvas headers.

The explicit qtcad BSG cast adapter is now private as well.  The installed
`include/qtcad/QgLegacyViewBsg.h` header has been removed from the public
header list, and the adapter lives in `src/libqtcad/QgLegacyViewBsg.h`
without exported declarations.  `QgLegacyView.cpp`, the transitional qtgl and
swrast libdm framebuffer windows, and the focused qtcad tests include that
private header through implementation/test include paths.  The pivot guard now
enforces that the adapter does not return to the installed qtcad headers and
that the private copy stays non-exported.

The normal qtcad Obol workflow tests have also moved off the direct qtcad BSG
view adapter where they only needed active-view and transaction-view setup.
`test_qtcad_obol_draw_sync`, selection sync, overlay sync, pick, measure,
export, and real-model coverage now call `qg_legacy_view_ged_active_set` and,
where they build explicit GED draw transactions,
`qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_view_set` instead of including
`QgLegacyViewBsg.h` or calling `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`.  The draw-sync test's
center/scale setup now uses qtcad legacy-view helpers as well, backed by a
new `qg_legacy_view_scale_set` wrapper.  The pivot guard rejects raw BSG view
setup from returning to that migrated test subset.

The qtcad snap and faceplate-sync workflow tests have now moved off the direct
qtcad BSG view adapter as well.  `QgLegacyView.h` exposes
`qg_legacy_view_snap_source_db_set`, `qg_legacy_view_size_set`, and
`qg_legacy_view_view2model_set` so the snap fixture can set DB snap source,
view size, center-query dimensions, and view-to-model state through the opaque
qtcad helper surface; snap result/current-point checks and faceplate ADC, grid,
model-axes, and view-axes get/set setup now use qtcad legacy-view helpers too.
The pivot guard rejects direct `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`,
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, raw `struct bsg_view`, `ged_gvp`, transaction `.view`,
direct `rt_view_*_bsg`, and `RT_VIEW_SNAP_*` usage from returning to those
tests.  qsketch's retained edit/scene graph calls still use the private BSG
bridge because they exercise lower-level retained state helpers directly.

The QgSW swrast integration test has also moved off direct qtcad BSG view
access for its normal view binding path.  `QgLegacyView.h` now exposes
`qg_legacy_view_scene_attached`, and `ged_test_qged_swrast.cpp` uses the
opaque helper surface for active GED view binding, GED view-set registration,
unit conversion, and the retained scene-attached assertion.  The pivot guard
now rejects direct `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`,
`qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, `struct bsg_view`, `ged_gvp`,
`rt_view_set_add_view_bsg`, `rt_view_unit_conversion_set_bsg`, and
`bsg_view_scene_attached` usage from returning to that test.

The standalone qsketch fixture now owns its staged view as an opaque
`qg_legacy_view *` as well.  `QgLegacyView.h` exposes
`qg_legacy_view_local_destroy` for callers that need retained view teardown,
and `qsketch.cpp` uses qtcad legacy-view helpers for local view lifecycle,
AET/scale/dimension setup, fit-to-view center and size updates, and grid state
get/set.  Its remaining retained edit creation and field-backed sketch
line-set scene nodes now route through the private
`src/libqtcad/QgLegacyViewSketch.h` bridge implemented in `QgLegacyView.cpp`.
That bridge owns the transitional `rt_edit_create_bsg`, retained scene-root,
node, and line-set calls, while qsketch holds only an opaque
`qg_legacy_view_sketch_lines *`.  The FreeBSD/clang hidden-visibility build
also confirmed that the private bridge functions must use `QTCAD_EXPORT`
because qsketch links against the shared `libqtcad` target even though the
header is not installed.  The pivot guard now rejects `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`,
`rt/edit_legacy_bsg.h`, `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, direct `rt_view_*_bsg` view
setup/free/update/grid calls, `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`, raw BSG node/line-set
refs, the old `m_bv` member path, and `qg_legacy_view_from_bsg` from returning
to qsketch.

The currently inactive retained-DM backend benchmark has also moved its view
setup and draw timing loop onto the qtcad private DM bridge.  `QgLegacyViewDm.h`
now exposes `qg_legacy_view_display_manager_get`, and
`ged_test_dm_backend_bench.cpp` uses `qg_legacy_view_ged_active_set`,
`qg_legacy_view_ged_view_set_add`, `qg_legacy_view_unit_conversion_set`,
`qg_legacy_view_dm_background_restore`, `qg_legacy_view_dm_draw`, and
`qg_legacy_view_dm_draw_end` instead of including `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`,
including `QgLegacyViewBsg.h`, casting through `qg_legacy_view_to_bsg`,
writing `ged_gvp`, calling direct RT legacy view setters, or calling direct
`dm_*` draw/background functions.  If the benchmark is re-enabled, its
commented CMake include path now names `src/libqtcad` for the private bridge
header.  The pivot guard locks that qtcad bridge route.

The standalone libdm qtgl/swrast framebuffer wrappers no longer include the
RT legacy-BSG view adapter or convert their qtcad canvas view to `bsg_view *`
for framebuffer setup.  `fb-qtgl.cpp` now sets framebuffer mode through
`qg_legacy_view_framebuffer_mode_set`, while `fb-swrast.cpp` uses qtcad
legacy-view helpers for framebuffer mode, dimensions, and swrast attach via
`qg_legacy_view_dm_open_swrast`.  The swrast canvas local view is already
initialized by `qg_legacy_view_local_create`, so the extra direct
`rt_view_init_bsg` call in the framebuffer wrapper was removed.  The pivot
guard now bans those direct RT/BSG setup calls from returning to the
framebuffer wrappers and requires the qtcad helper route instead.

The qtgl display-manager context guard has been moved behind the RT legacy
view adapter as well.  `dm-qtgl.cpp` previously included `bsg/defines.h`,
cast the incoming context to `struct bsg_view *`, and checked
`BSG_VIEW_MAGIC` directly to reject the retained default view context.  The new
`rt_view_context_is_bsg` helper keeps that magic-value knowledge inside
`rt/view_legacy_bsg`, and the qtgl DM now calls the helper instead of spelling
BSG types or constants.  `rt_view_legacy_bsg` has direct unit coverage for the
null and valid-view cases, and the pivot guard rejects direct qtgl BSG context
checks from returning.

The swrast display-manager private context has also stopped exposing the
retained BSG view type.  `dm-swrast.h` now stores the retained view context as
an opaque pointer, while `dm-swrast.cpp` synchronizes width and height through
`rt_view_context_width_from_bsg`, `rt_view_context_height_from_bsg`, and
`rt_view_context_dimensions_set_bsg`.  The RT legacy adapter tests cover the
null and valid context-helper cases, and the pivot guard now rejects a return
to direct BSG view dimension calls or `struct bsg_view` exposure in swrast.

The migrated qtcad Obol workflow tests have moved their retained draw
appearance setup behind the qtcad legacy-view boundary as well.
`QgLegacyView.h` now exposes the opaque
`qg_legacy_view_draw_appearance` handle plus create/destroy and transaction
setter helpers, and `QgLegacyView.cpp` owns the backing
`bsg_appearance_settings` storage required by the current GED draw transaction
implementation.  Draw sync, selection sync, pick, measure, snap, and real-model
tests no longer include `ged/bsg_ged_draw.h`; the shaded/wire tests use
`QG_LEGACY_VIEW_DRAW_MODE_*` values through the qtcad wrapper and no longer
spell `BSG_DRAW_MODE_*`, `BSG_APPEARANCE_SETTINGS_INIT`, direct
`bsg_appearance_settings`, or transaction `.appearance` writes.  The pivot
guard now requires the wrapper helpers and rejects those retained appearance
tokens from returning to the migrated qtcad workflow tests.  Verification on

The qtcad workflow tests have also moved their draw observer registration and

The migrated qtcad workflow tests now route draw transaction construction
through the qtcad legacy-view boundary too.  `QgLegacyView.h` exposes
`qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_init` and
`qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_paths_set`, with `QgLegacyView.cpp` mapping
the qtcad transaction-kind enum to retained `GED_DRAW_TXN_*` values and
owning the borrowed multi-path field writes.  Draw sync, selection sync, pick,
measure, snap, and real-model tests now use `qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction`
locals, `QG_LEGACY_VIEW_DRAW_TXN_*` values, and the wrapper paths setter
instead of spelling `struct ged_draw_transaction`,
`ged_draw_transaction_make`, `GED_DRAW_TXN_*`, or transaction `.paths` /
`.path_count` assignments.  The pivot guard now rejects those raw construction
tokens from returning to the migrated workflow tests.  Verification on

`QgLegacyView.cpp` now also self-routes its retained draw convenience helpers
through that wrapper surface.  `qg_legacy_view_draw_redraw`,

`qg_legacy_view_draw_erase_path_apply` use

init/free, and `qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_apply` instead of duplicating
direct `ged_draw_transaction_make`, `ged_draw_apply_transaction`, and

The qged framebuffer-server update path now uses the same private qtcad
retained-DM bridge for framebuffer-backed canvas lookup.  `QgLegacyViewDm.h`
exposes exported private helpers for converting a raw framebuffer pointer to
the opaque `qg_legacy_fb` handle, recovering the qtgl/swrast Qt canvas used
for update signals, and forwarding DM init messages.  `fbserv.cpp` includes
that private bridge instead of `dm.h`, and its TCP and IPC client-open paths no
longer call `fb_get_dm`, `dm_get_ctx`, `dm_get_udata`, or `dm_init_msgs`
directly.  The qged target now has `src/libqtcad` on its include path for this
private bridge, and the pivot guard requires the bridge route while rejecting
those raw DM lookup calls from returning to qged.

Building `qged` on FreeBSD/clang also exposed a stale retained-view access in
the tkswrast plugin: `dm-tkswrast.cpp` still referenced the removed
`swrast_vars::v` member after the swrast private context was made opaque.

by `rt_view_legacy_bsg`, and tkswrast uses that plus
`rt_view_context_dimensions_set_bsg` through `swrast_vars::view_ctx` for its
refresh and dimension updates.  The pivot guard now requires the context
helper route and rejects direct `sv->v`, raw refresh, or raw dimension helper
use in tkswrast.

The installed qtcad legacy-view draw API no longer aliases retained GED draw

helpers to translate qtcad records to retained GED transactions at apply time
and to copy retained GED results back into qtcad results.  Draw observer
registration now allocates a small qtcad bridge object; the retained GED
observer callback is translated to qtcad transaction/result records before
calling qtcad/qged observers, and observer removal unregisters the retained
token and frees the bridge.  Raw retained GED transaction/result types and

The qtcad legacy-view draw transaction storage is now opaque too.  The

write through `qg_legacy_view_draw_transaction_autoview_set`.  Existing
migrated callers still stack-allocate transaction records and use the qtcad
init/setter/accessor functions; the pivot guard now requires the opaque
storage form and rejects the old public transaction fields from returning to
the installed header.

The qged framebuffer-server header boundary is now tighter than the earlier
forward-declare cleanup.  `src/qged/fbserv.h` no longer names

owns the cast back to `fbserv_obj`, the retained `dm/fbserv.h` include, and
the callback signatures that populate GED's retained framebuffer-server table.

The retained view display-manager pointer now has an RT legacy adapter
boundary.  `rt_view_display_manager_from_bsg` and

callers to write `struct bsg_view::dmp` directly.  MGED command forwarding,

The display-manager adapter cleanup now covers the libged DM command path as
well.  `src/libged/dm/dm.c` uses a local typed helper backed by
`rt_view_display_manager_from_bsg` for DM lookup/list/attach checks and uses
`rt_view_display_manager_set_bsg` when attach succeeds, leaving no direct
`dmp` field access in that production file.  TclCAD new-view creation now sets
the newly opened display manager through the RT legacy setter and reads it
through the getter for the immediate pathname handoff; the larger TclCAD DM
read cleanup remains separate.  The pivot guard now enforces the strict
libged DM boundary and rejects new direct TclCAD display-manager field writes.

While running the focused drawing tests on FreeBSD/clang, the swrast/qtgl DM
plugins failed to load because the private qtcad canvas bridge factory methods
were not exported from `libqtcad.so`.  `QgCanvasBridgeFactory` is now marked
with `QTCAD_EXPORT`, and the pivot guard requires that export marker so
`libdm-swrast.so` and `libdm-qtgl.so` can resolve the bridge symbols at
runtime.

Verification on FreeBSD/clang passed with:
`ninja -C build libqtcad dm-swrast dm-qtgl libged libtclcad bin/mged
rt_view_legacy_bsg`, a dynamic-symbol check showing exported

`src/libged/dm/screengrab.c`, `src/libged/selection_state.cpp`, and
`src/gtools/gsh/gsh.cpp` now route active-view reads, named-view lookup, and
view-set iteration through `ged_view_active_ctx`, `ged_view_find_ctx`, and
`ged_view_set_views_ctx` instead of reading `gedp->ged_gvp`,
`gedp->ged_views`, or calling `rt_view_set_views_bsg`/
`rt_view_set_find_view_bsg` directly.

The `who` command paths now keep their view handles opaque as GED draw
contexts and no longer include `rt/view_legacy_bsg.h`.  The retained RT
view-operation calls in `zap`, `draw`, `erase`, `screengrab`, and `gsh` remain
transitional, but they now consume views obtained from the neutral GED
accessor boundary.  The pivot guard now requires the neutral accessor tokens in
this migrated libged/gsh batch, removes stale `rt_view_set_*_bsg` expectations
from those files, rejects direct `ged_gvp`/`ged_views` reach-through there, and
no longer requires the legacy RT view header in files that have stopped using
it.

FreeBSD/clang also exposed a strict declaration issue while rebuilding the
geometry shell: `src/gtools/gsh/gsh.cpp` used `ged_draw_scene_hash` without
including its declaration owner.  `gsh.cpp` now includes
`ged/bsg_ged_draw.h` explicitly.

exposes `rt_view_context_aet_set_bsg` and `rt_view_context_update_bsg`,
implemented as delegating context wrappers over the existing typed AET and
view-update adapters, and `src/librt/tests/view_legacy_bsg.c` covers their
null and live-view behavior.

`src/libged/tests/draw/aet.cpp` now uses `ged_view_set_ctx`,
`ged_view_set_views_ctx`, `ged_view_active_ctx_set`,
`rt_view_context_create_with_set_bsg`, `rt_view_set_context_remove_bsg`,
`rt_view_set_context_add_bsg`, `rt_view_context_name_set_bsg`, and opaque RT
view-context setup helpers for display-manager access/storage, live scale
storage, dimension setup, unit conversion, AET writes, and view updates.  It
still intentionally exercises retained draw transactions and `dm_draw_objs`,
but those calls now receive opaque view contexts instead of locally named
`struct bsg_view` pointers.

The pivot guard now requires the new context AET/update wrappers in the RT
adapter header, implementation, adapter test, and `aet.cpp`; rejects direct
`gedp->ged_gvp`, `struct bsg_view`, `v->dmp`, `gv_name`, typed RT view-set,
dimension, unit-conversion, scale-storage, AET, and update calls from
returning to the AET fixture; and rejects a direct libbsg relink for
`ged_test_aet`.
